1b1(khri pa): @##//rgya gar rnams hA 
1b2(khri pa): Ar+ya dang sha rdzas ha 
1b3(khri pa): sri ka pradz+nyA pA ra mi tA nA 
2a1(khri pa): @##//ma hA yA na sU tA/bod skad 
2a2(khri pa): du 'khrugs pa shes rab kyi pha rol 
2a3(khri pa): tu phyin pa khri pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mngo 
2b1(khri pa): bam po dang po/sangs rgyas dang phyag 'tshal
2b2(khri pa): dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal
2b3(khri pa): lo'adi skad bdag gis thos 
2b4(khri pa): pa dus gcig na/bcem ldan 
xa1(khri pa): ye shes chen po ldan pa yi//tshig dag la ni mi rten par//ci'i phyir na tshang bar lta//dge slong rnams ni phan gdags phyir//khyod la bden tshig smra ba yin//tshad smra ngan 'gror 'gro ba'i mi//yang dag tshangs spyod ma 
xa2(khri pa): yin pas//de ni rgyal po'i pho brang du//btsun mo dag dang lan cig tu//myur zhing rings par pho brang dong //de tshe dri med pho brang nang //chos rnams rang bzhin stong pa dang //bdag dang sems can med pa yi//
xa3(khri pa): dam pa'i don ni ston par byed//rgyal po dmag 'khor bcas pa rnams//thams cad bdud kyis bslus pas na//pho brang nang gi btsun mo rnams//dge slong yongs bskor mthong ba dang //zhe sdang glang 
xa4(khri pa): chen myos pa bzhin//gshed ma dag la rab bsgo ba//dge slong nga yi btsun mo bslus//sdug bsngal chos kyi chad pas chod//blon po 'khor dang bcas pa rnams//thams chad bdud 
xa5(khri pa): kyis zil pa na//dge slong nyes pa med pa la//sems kyis gnod pa bya bar bsams//gshed ma mtshon thogs pa dang //dri med snying brtse mchi ma byung //rgyal pos khyod ni chos min na//ci yi 
xa6(khri pa): phyir na mchi ma byung //dri med rgyal po la smras pa//dngos po 'di ni yid ches dka'//re shig skad cig sdod cig dang //bdag la gsad pa'i dbang yod do//rgyal pos dge slong chig thos nas//gshed ma 
xa7(khri pa): dag la sdod cig bsgo//bden pa'i dngos po ci zhig yod//khyod kyis myur du smra ba'i rigs//lhag pa'i bsam pa ldan 'gyur zhing //snying rjes 'jig rten phan 'dogs pas//ser mo bcu'i thal sbyar te//
xa8(khri pa): bden pa'i tshig ni 'di skad smras//rgyal po chen pos mkhyen par mdzod//gal te yang dag nyes med na//sa 'di rnam pa drug tu g.yos//nam mkha' la me tog char phob shig/de skad tshig ni smras ma thag/
3a1(khri pa): @##//'das rgyal po'i khab bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri la dge slong stong 
3a2(khri pa): phrag du ma'i dge 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/thams cad kyang dgra bcom pa/zag 
3a3(khri pa): pa zad pa/nyon mongs pa med pa/dbang dang ldan par gyur pa/sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/
3a4(khri pa): shes rab shin tu rnam par grol ba cang shes pa/glang po che na po/bya ba byas pa/byed pa 
3a5(khri pa): byas pa/khur bor ba/bdag gi don rjes su thob pa/srid par kun tu sbyor ba/yongs su zad pa/yang dag pa'i shes 
3b1(khri pa): pas sems shin tu rnam grol ba/sems can thams cad kyi dbang gi dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa bshad sta/gang zag 
3b2(khri pa): gcig ma gtogs pa ni 'di lta ste/slob pa rgyun tu zhugs pa tshe dang ldan pa dga' bo'o//dge slong ma lnga brgya tsam dang /
3b3(khri pa): dge bsnyen dang /dge bsnyen ma thams cad kyang bden pa mthong ba sha stag dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
3b4(khri pa): chen po stong phrag du ma thams cad kyang gzungs thob pa/ting nge 'dzin thob pa/stong pa nyid la gnas pa/mtshan ma med pa'i spyod yul ba/mi 
3b5(khri pa): brtags pa'i smon lam dang ldan pa/mnyam pa nyid kyi bzod pa thob pa/spobs pa thogs pa med pa thob pa/thams cad kyang mngon par shes pa 
3b6(khri pa): lnga dang ldan pa/gzung ba'i tshig dang ldan pa/tshul 'chos pa med pa/shes kyi khe dang /rnyed pa dang /bkur bsti'i sems dang bral ba/zang zing 
4a1(khri pa): @##//med pa'i chos ston pa/chos zab mo la bzod pa'i pha rol tu son pa/mi 'jigs pa thob pa/bdud kyi las las yang dag par 'das pa/las kyi sgrib pa thams cad las rnam par grol ba/
4a2(khri pa): rten pa'i chos ston pas bsags pa/bskal pa grangs med pa nas smon lam shin tu bsags pa/gzhin 'dzum pa dang ldan pa/gsong por smra ba/gzhin khro gnyer med pa/'khor mtha' yas pa/zil gyis gnon 
4a3(khri pa): pa'i mi 'jigs pa dang ldan pa/bskal pa bye ba phrag du mar bstan pas nges par 'byung ba la mkhas pa/chos la sgyu ma dang /smig rgyu dang /rmi lam dang /chu zla dang /mig yor dang /nam mkha' dang /brag cha dang /dri za'i grong khyer dang /
4a4(khri pa): gzugs brnyan dang /sprul pa lta bur mos pa/mi 'jigs pa tshad med pa dang ldan pa/sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi spyod pa dang /mos pa dang /phra ba mkhyen pa/khong du chud par bya ba la mkhas pa/sems can 
4a5(khri pa): thams cad dang zhe 'gras pa med pa'i sems dang ldan pa/bzod pa chen po dang ldan pa/ji lta ba bzhin du 'jug pa la mkhas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas par smon lam yongs su bzung ba/'jig rten 
4a6(khri pa): gyi khams grangs med pa'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i ting nge 'dzin rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par mngon du gyur pa/sangs rgyas dpag tu med pa la gsol pa 'debs pa la mkhas pa/lta ba sna tshogs kyis kun nas ldang ba'i nyon mongs 
4a7(khri pa): pa spong ba la mkhas pa/ting nge 'dzin gyis rnam par rtse ba 'bum mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sha stag la 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bzang skyong dang /dkon mchog 
4a8(khri pa): 'byung gnas dang /ded dpon dang /na las byin dang /gzas byin dang /chu lhas byin dang /dbang pos byin dang /blo gros cha chen dang /pho nya la dang /bcas pa dang /bcas pa dang /
4b1(khri pa): dang /rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa dang /rtag tu rtson dang /brtson pa mi gtong ba dang /nyi ma'i snying po dang /zla ba nyi ma nyi ma nyi ma nyi ma ni bcu bzhi pa dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag du ma thams cad kyang gzhan nur gyur pa gta
4b2(khri pa): che dang /byang chub sems dpa' rtag tu lag brkyang dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /de dag la sog pa byang chub sems dpa'ang stong phrag du ma thams can kyang gzhan nur gyur pas /ta
4b3(khri pa): dang thabs cig go /de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba thams cad kyi gzi brjid zil gyis mnan nas /'khor bzhi po dag gi mdun na lham me/lhan ne lhang nge zhing rdzu
4b4(khri pa): 'phrul gyis rnam par 'phrul pa'i chos 'phrul rnam pa du ma yang dag par bstan to//spu'i khung bu rnams nas kyang 'od zer bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag du ma dag byung bar gyur to//yang de'i tshe cha 
4b5(khri pa): dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'khor der 'dus par gyur te/'dug pa des/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba'i cho 'phrul de lta bu mthong nas/dga' zhing mgu la yid rangs te/rab tu
4b6(khri pa): 'gu zhing dga' ba dang /yid bde ba skyes nas stan las langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzar nas/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha ngas la gtsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud
4b7(khri pa): nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//gal te zhus nas zhu ba lung bstan pa'i slad du/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs phye na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'da'as la
4b8(khri pa): bdag phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu lagaso//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ta'i bu la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//shA ra dwa ti'i bu khyod de bzhin
5a1(khri pa): @#//gshegs pa la zhu ba'i phyir/rtag tu skabs phye zin gyis/khyod de bzhin gshegs pa la ci dang /ci 'dod pa dris shig dang /dris pa de dang de'i lan btab pas khyod kyi sems 
5a2(khri pa): rangs par bya'o//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bus bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
5a3(khri pa): phyin pa zhes bgyi na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa/chos gang gi pha rol tu phyin pas byang chub sems dpa' rnas kyi shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa
5a4(khri pa): pha rol tu son pa lags shing /myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar yang 'gyur la gas/de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das 
5a5(khri pa): kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA radwati'i bu la legaso zhes bya ba byin te/shA radwati'i bu legaso legaso//'di ltar khyod de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi brlabs kyis zhu ba zhus pa mdzes so//
5a6(khri pa): khyod kyis yongs su rtogs pa bzang ngo//de'i phyir khyod legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zug shig dang /shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyinapa bstan par bya'o//btsun pa de ltar 'tshalo zhes gsol te/
5a7(khri pa): tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bu/bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan pa dang /bcom ldan 'angs kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//shA radwati'i bu shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/chos thams cad 
5a8(khri pa): la mngon par zhen pa med pa yin no//shA radwati'i bu byang chub sems dpa'i mngon par zhen pa med rnams ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i phyir 
5b1(khri pa): byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar yang 'gyur ro//gsol ba/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub tu 
5b2(khri pa): thams cad gang lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA radwa ti'i bu chos thams cad ces bya ba ni 'di lta ste/phung po lnga dang /skya ba dang zhu gnyas dang /7khams bca brgyad dang /
5b3(khri pa): bden pa bzhi dang /rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba'i yan lag bcu gnyis dang /dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi dang /yang dag par spong ba bzhi dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi dang /dbang po lnga dang /stobs 
5b4(khri pa): lnga dang /byang chub kyi yan lag 'dun dang /'phags pa'i la ma yan brgyad pa dang /rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /rnam par thar ba'i sgo mtshan ma med pa dang /rnam par tharapa'i sgo 
5b5(khri pa): smon pa med pa dang /bsam gtan bzhi dang /tshad med pa bzhi dang /nga /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi dang /rnam par thar pa brgyad dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 
5b6(khri pa): 'jug pa dgu dang /'du shes dgu dang /rjesu dran pa bcu dang /'du shes kyi rnam pa drug dang /chos shes pa dang /rjesu rtogs pa shes pa dang /pha rol gyi sems shes pa dang /kun rdzob shes pa dang /sdug 
5b7(khri pa): bsngal shes pa dang /kun 'byung shes pa dang /'gog pa shes pa dang /lam shes pa dang /zad pa shes pa dang /mi skye ba shes pa dang /ji lta ba bzhin shes pa dang /de bzhin du rtog pa dang bcas/
5b8(khri pa): dpyod pa dang bcas pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /rtog pa med pa la dpyod pa tsam gyi ting nge 'dzin dang /mi rtog mi dpyod pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang mi shes pa kun shes par byed pa'i dbang po dang /kun shes pa'i dbang po dang /kun
6a1(khri pa): shes pa dang ldan pa'i dbang po dang /zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mched brgyad dang /zad par gyi skye mched bcu dang /stong pa nyid bco brgyad dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs 
6a2(khri pa): bcu dang /mi 'jigs pa bzhi dang /so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi dang /byams pa chen po dang /snying rje chen po dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad dang /de bzhIn du thams cad shes 
6a3(khri pa): pa nyid dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang /pha rol tu phyin pa drug dang /mngon par shes pa lnga dang /mig lnga dang /skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa 
6a4(khri pa): gnyis dang /dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu po dag ste/gang la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis mngon par zhen par mi bya ba'i chos thams cad ni de dag yin no//mngon 
6a5(khri pa): par zhen pa med pa ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i pha rol tu son pa yin zhing /myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar yang 'gyur ro//
6a6(khri pa): de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bus 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das phung po lnga gang lags shes bya ba nas/rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba yan lag bcu gnyis pa'i bar 
6a7(khri pa): du de bzhin du sbyar ro//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi gang lags zhes bya ba nas/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar du de bzhin du sbyar ro/rnam par thar pa'i sngo sdong pa nyid gang lags shes pa 
6a8(khri pa): nas/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du de bzhin du sbyar ra ro//pha rol tu phyin pa drug gang lags shes pa bcas 
6b1(khri pa): skad ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bu la/'di skad ces bkag
6b2(khri pa): shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa'o//de la skye mched bcu gnyis gang zhe na/nang gi drug dang /phya' drug sta/da dag
6b3(khri pa): drug gang zhe na/'di lta ste/mig gi skye mched dang /rna ba'i skye mched dang /sna'i skye mched dang /lce'i skye mched dang /lus kyi skye mched dang /yid kyi skye mched de/de dag ni nang gi skye mched 
6b4(khri pa): drug ces bya'o//de la phyi'i skye mched drug gang zhe na/'di lta/ste/gzugs kyi skye mched dang /sgra'i skye mched dang /dri'i skye mched dang /ro'i skye mched dang /reg bya'i skye mched 
6b5(khri pa): dang /chos kyi skye mched de/de dgani phyi'i skye mched drug ces bya'o//de la khams bco brgyad gang zhe na/'di lta /ste/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /
6b6(khri pa): mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /rna ba'i khams dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /sna'i khams dang /dri'i khams dang /sna'i rnam par shes pa'i khams
6b7(khri pa): lce'i khams dang /ro'i khams dang /lce'i rnam par shes pa'i khamasadang /lus kyi khams dang /reg bya'i khams dang /lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khamsdang /yid kyi khams dang /chos kyi khams dang /
6b8(khri pa): yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i kham te/de dag ni khams bco brgyad ces bya'o//de la 'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sdug bsngal 'phags pa'i bden pa dang /kun 'byung 'phags
7a1(khri pa): pa'i bden pa dang /'gog pa 'phags pa'i bden pa dang /lam 'phags pa'i bden pa ste/de dag ni 'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi zhes bya'o//de la rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba yan lag 
7a2(khri pa): bcu gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/ma rig pa'i rkyen gyis 'du byed rnams/'du byed kyi rkyen gyis rnam par shes pa/rnam par shes pa'i rkyen gyis ming dang gzugs/ming dang gzugs kyi rkyen gyis skye mched 
7a3(khri pa): drug/skye mched drug gi rkyen gyis reg pa/reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba/tshor ba'i rkyen gyis sred pa/sred pa'i rkyen gyis ba len pa/len pa'i rkyen gyis srid pa/srid pa'i rkyen gyis skye ba/skye ba'i 
7a4(khri pa): rkyen gyis rga shi dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba dang /'khrug pa rnams 'byung ste/de ltar na sdug bsngal gyi phung po chen po 'ba' shig po 
7a5(khri pa): 'di dag 'byung bar 'gyur ro//ma rig pa 'gags pas 'du byed 'a gag/'du byed 'gags pas rnam par shes pa 'gag/rnam par shes pa 'gags pas/ming dang gzugs 
7a6(khri pa): 'gag/ming dang gzugs 'gags pas/skye mched drug 'gag/skye mched drug 'gags pas/reg pa 'gag/reg pa 'gags pas/tshor ba 'gag/tshor ba 'gags pas sred pa 'gag/
7a7(khri pa): sred pa 'gags pas/len pa 'gag/len pa 'gags pas/srid pa 'gag/srid pa 'gags pas/skye ba 'gags/skye ba 'gags/shi 'gags/shi 'gags pas/shi 'gags pas//
7a8(khri pa): ngan dang /smre sngags 'dod pa dang /sdug bsngal ba ba dang /yid mi bde pa dang /'khrug pa rnams la 'gag par 'gyur ba de la 'gags pas//skye ba 'gags pas/skye ba 'gag pas/shi 'gags pas shi 'gags pas//
7b1(khri pa): rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba yan lag bcu gnyis pa lugs dang /mthun pa dang /lugs dang mi 'thun pa zhes 
7b2(khri pa): bar gzhag pa dang /tshor ba la tshor ba'i rjes su lta ba'i dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dang /sems la sems kyi rjes su lta ba'i dran pa nyer gzhag pa dang /chos lam gcig tu rta bgrag//////
7b3(khri pa): nye bar gzhag pa bzhi zhes bya'o//de la yang dag par spong ba bzhi gang zhe na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sdig pa mi dge ba'i chos ma skyes pa rnams mi bskyed pa'i phyir/'dun pa bskyed cing /
7b4(khri pa): 'bad la brtson 'grus rtsom ste/sems rab tu 'dzin cing /yang dag par rab tu 'jog pa dang /sdig pa mi dge ba'i chos skyes pa rnams spang ba'i phyir 'dun pa bskyed cing /'bad la brtson 'grus 
7b5(khri pa): rtsom ste/sems rab tu 'dzi cing /yang dag par rab/tu 'jog pa dang /dge ba'i chos ma skyes pa rnal bskyed pa'i phyir 'dun pa bskyed cig/'bad la brtson 'grus rtsoms ste/sems 
7b6(khri pa): rab tu 'dzin cing yang dag par rab tu 'jig pa dang /dge ba'i chos/skyes pa rnams gnas pa dang /mi brjed pa dang /shin tu rgyas pa dang /phyir zhing bsgom pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir 'dun pa bskyed
7b7(khri pa): cing /'bad la brtson 'gyus rtsom ste/sems rab tu mdzin cing /yang dag par reg tu 'jog pa ste/de dag ni yang dag pa'i spong ba bzhi zhes bya'o//de la rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/
7b8(khri pa): 'dun pa'i ting nge 'dzin spong ba'i 'du byed dang ldan pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi ting nge 'dzin spong ba'i du byed dang ldan pa'i rdzu phrul gyi rkang pa dang /sems kyi ting nge 'dzin spongs ba'i 'du byed dang ldan
8a1(khri pa): pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang /dpyod pa'i tinge 'dzin spong ba'i 'du byed dang ldan pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang ba ste/de dag ni rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang ba bzhi zhes bya'o//de la dbang po lnga gang zhe na/'di lta 
8a2(khri pa): ste dad pa'i dbang po dang /brtson 'grus kyi dbang po dang /dran pa'i dbang po dang /tinge 'dzin gyi dbang po dang /shes rab kyi dbang po ste/de dag ni dbang po lnga zhes bya'o//de la stobs lnga gang zhe na/'di lta ste/
8a3(khri pa): de bzhin du dad pa'i stobs dang /brtson 'grus kyi stobs dang /dran pa'i stobs dang /tinge 'dzin gyi stobs dang /shes rab kyi stobs te/de dag ni stobs lnga zhes bya'o//de la byang chub kyi yan lag bdun 
8a4(khri pa): gang zhe na/'di lta ste/dran pa yang dag byang chub kyi yan lag dang /chos rab tu rnam par 'byed pa yang dag byang chub kyi lan lag dang /brtson 'grus yang dag byang chub kyi yanalag dang /
8a5(khri pa): dga' ba yang dag byang chub kyi yan//shin tu sbyangs pa yang dag byang chub kyi yin lag dang /tinge 'dzin yang dag byang chub kyi yan
8a6(khri pa): lag ste/de dag ni byang chub kyi yan lag bdu zhes bya'o//de la 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa gang zhe na/'di lta ste yang dag pa'i lta ba dang /yang dag pa'i rtog pa dang /yang dag pa'i ngag dang /yang 
8a7(khri pa): dag pa'i las kyi mtha' dang /yang dag pa'i 'tsho ba dang /yang dag pa'i rtsol ba dang /yang dag pa'i dran pa dang /yang dag pa'itinge 'dzin te/de dag ni 'phags pa'i lam lag ba tshad pa zhes 
8a8(khri pa): de la rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid gang zhe na/'di lta ste/chos thams cad rang gi mtshan nyid kyis stong par so sor rtog pa'i sems gnas 
8b1(khri pa): stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la rnam par thar pa'i sgo mtshan ma med pa gang zhe na/'di lta ste/chos thams cad rang gzhan
8b2(khri pa): mtshan ma med pa ste/de ni rnam par thar pa'i sgo mtshad ma med pa zhes bya'o//de la rnam par thor pa'i sgo smon pa med pa gang zhe na/'di la lta ste/chos thams cad
8b3(khri pa): med par sems gnas pa smon pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa'i sgo ste/de ni rnam par thar ba'i sgo smon pa zhes bya'o//de la bsam gtan bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste /'dod pa dag las dben pa/sdig pa mi dge ba'i chos
8b4(khri pa): rnams las dben pa/rtog pa dang bcas pa/dpyod pa dang bcas pa/dben pa las skyes pa'i dga' ba dang /bde ba can bsam gtan dang po bsgrubs te gnas pa dang /de rtog pa dang dpyod pa dang bral
8b5(khri pa): zhing nang yongasu dang ste/sems kyi rgyud gcig tu gyur pas rtog pa med pa dpyod pa med pa'i tinge 'dzin las skyes pa'i dga' ba dag/bde ba can bsam gtanaganyis pa bsgrubs te gnas pa dang/de
8b6(khri pa): dga' ba'i 'dod chags dang bral bas gtang snyoms la gnas shing dran pa dang /shes bzhin can yin te/bde ba lus kyis myong la 'phags pa rnams kyis gang de dran pa dang ldan bde ba la gnas pa gtang snyoms
8b7(khri pa): ba'o zhes brjod pa ste/dga' ba med pa'i bsam gtan gsum pa bsgrubs te gnas pa dag/de bde ba yang spangs te/snga nas sdug bsngal yang spangs shing /yid bde ba dang /yid mi bde ba yang nub pas
8b8(khri pa): sdug bsngal yang ma yin/bde ba yang ma yin/btang snyoms dang dran pa yongasu dag pas/bsam gtan bzhi pa bsgrubs nas gnas pa ste/de dag ni bsam gtan bzhi zhes bya'o//de la tshad med pa
9a1(khri pa): pa bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/byams pa dang /snying rje dang /dga' ba dang /btang snyoms te/de dag ni tshad med pa bzhi zhes bya'o//de la gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 
9a2(khri pa): 'jug pa bzhi gang zhe na/'di blta ste/nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mched dang /rnam shes mtha' yas skye mched dang/ci yang med pa'i skye mched dang /'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched de/de dag ni 
9a3(khri pa): gzugs med pa'i snyoms pa 'jug pa bzhi zhes bya'o//de rnam par thar pa brgyad gang zhe na/'di lta ste/gzugs can gzugs rnams la lta ba 'di ni rnam par thar pa dang po'o//gzugs med par 'du shes 
9a4(khri pa): pas phyi rol gyi gzugs rnams la lta ba nyid ni rnam par thar pa gnyis pa'o//sdug pa nyid du mos pa 'di ni/rnam par thar pa gsum pa'o//de la rnam pa thams cad du gzugs kyi 'du shes 
9a5(khri pa): rnams las yang dag par 'das te/thogs pa'i 'du shes rnams nub cing /sna tshogs kyi 'du shes rnams yid la mi byed pas/nam mkha' mtha' yas so snyam nas/nam mkha' 
9a6(khri pa): mtha' yas skye mched bsgrubs te gnas pa 'di ni/rnam par thal ba bzhi po'o//de rnam pa thams cad du nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mched las yang dag par 'das ste/rnam shes mtha' yas so snyam 
9a7(khri pa): nas/rnam shes mtha' yas skye mched bsgrub ste/gnas pa 'di ni/rnam pa thar pa lnga pa'o//de rnam pa thams can du rnam shes mtha' yas skye mchod las yang dag par 'das/ci yang 
9a8(khri pa): med do snyam nas ci yang med pa'i skye mched bsgrubs te gnasapa 'di ni/rnam par thar ba drug pa'o//de rnam pa thams chad du ci yang med pa'i skye 
9b1(khri pa): shes med min skye mched nye bar bsgrubs te/gnas pa 'di ni/rnam par thar pa bdun pa'o//de rnam pa thams cad du gtugs pa dang /
9b2(khri pa): dang /tshor ba 'gog pa bsgrubs te gnas pa 'di ni/rnam pa thar pa brgyad pa ste/de dag ni rnam par thar pa brgyad ces bya'o//de la mthar gyis gnas par snyams par 'jug go//
9b3(khri pa): lta ste 'dod pa dag las dben pa/sdig pa mi dge ba'i chos rnams las dben pa/rtog pa dang bcas pa/dpyod pa dang bcas pa/dben pa las skyes pa'i dga' ba dang /bde ba can bsam gtan dang po bsgrubs 
9b4(khri pa): te gnas pa 'di ni snyoms par 'jug pa dang po'o//de rtog pa dang /dpyod pa dang bral zhing /nang yongs su dang ste/sems kyi rgyud gcig tu gyur pas/rtog pa med pa dpyod pa med pa'i ting 
9b5(khri pa): 'dzin las skyes pa'i dga' ba dag/bde ba can bsam gtan/gnyis pa bsgrubs te gnas pa ni/snyoms par 'jug pa gnyis pa'o//de dga' ba'i 'dod chags dang bral bas 
9b6(khri pa): btang snyoms la gnas shing /dran pa dang /shes bzhin can te/bde ba lus kyis myong la 'phags pa rnams kyis gang de dran pa dang ldan pa/bde ba la gnas pa btang snyoms pa'o zhes brjod pa ste/
9b7(khri pa): dga' ba med pa'i bsam gtan gsum pa bsgrubs te/gnas pa 'di ni snyoms pa 'jug pa gsum pa'o//de bde ba yang spangs te/snga nas sdug bsngal yang spang shing /yid bde ba dang 
9b8(khri pa): yid mi bde ba nub pas/sdug bsngal yang ma yin/bde ba yang ma yin pa/btang snyoms dang /dran pa yongs su dag pa bsam gtan bzhi pa bsgrubs te/gnas pa'i 'di ni/snyoms 
10a1(khri pa): par 'jug pa bzhi pa'o//de rnam pa thams cad du gzugs kyi 'du shes rnams las yang dag par 'das te/thogs pa'i 'du shes rnams nub par gyur cing /sna tshogs kyi 
10a2(khri pa): 'du shes rnams yid la mi byed pas/nam mkha' mtha' yas so snyam nas/nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mched bsgrubs te gnas pa 'di ni/snyoms par 'jug pa lnga pa'o//de rnam 
10a3(khri pa): pa thams cad du nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mchid las yang dag par 'das/rnam par shes pa mtha' yas so snyam nas/rnam shes mtha' yas skye mched nye bar bsgrubs te/gnas pa 'di ni snyoms 
10a4(khri pa): par 'jug pa drug pa'o//de rnam pa thams chad du rnam shes mtha' yas skye mchid las dag par 'das te/ci yang med do snyam nas ci yang med pa'i skye mched bsgrubs te gnasapa 'di ni 
10a5(khri pa): snyoms par 'jug pa 'dun pa'o//de rnam pa thams cid du ci yang med pa'i skye mche las yang dag par 'das ste/'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched bsgrubs te gnas
10a6(khri pa): ni snyoms par 'jug pa brgyad pa'o//de rnams pa thams cad du 'du shes med 'du shes me min skye mched las yang dag par 'da' ste/'du shes dang /tshor ba 'gog pa bsgrubs te gnas pa 'di ni 
10a7(khri pa): snyoms par 'jog pa dgu pa ste/de dag ni mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu zhes bya'o//de la 'du shes dgu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/rnam par bam pa'i 'du shes dang /rnam par 
10a8(khri pa): 'bus gzhigs pa'i 'du shes dang /rnamapar dmar ba'i 'du shes dang /rnam pa rnam pa'i 'du shes dang /rnag par sngos pa'i 'du shes dang /
10b1(khri pa): par mthor ba'i 'du shes dang /rus gong gi 'du shes dang /rnam par tshig pa'i 'du shes te/de dag ni 'du shes dgu zhes bya'o/////
10b2(khri pa): pa dang /chos rjesu dran pa dang /dge 'dun rjesu dran pa dang /tshul khrims rjesu dran pa dang /gtong ba rjesu dran pa dang /lha rjesu dran pa dang /nye bar zhi ba rjesu dran pa dang /dbugs 
10b3(khri pa): 'byung ba dang /rngub pa rjes su dran pa dang /lus su gtogs pa rjesu dran pa dang /'chi ba rjesu dran pa ste/de dag ni rjesu dran pa bcu zhes bya'o//de la 'du shes kyi rnam pa drug gang zhe na/
10b4(khri pa): 'di lta ste/mi rtag pa'i 'du shes dang /sdug bsngal ba'i 'du shes dang /bdag med pa'i 'du shes dang /mi sdug pa'i 'du shes dang /'chi ba'i 'du shes dang /'jig 
10b5(khri pa): rten thams cad la mngon par mi dga' ba'i 'du shes dang /de dag ni 'du shes kyi rnam pa drug ces bya'o//de la chos shes pa gang zhe na/phung po lnga po rnams yongasu sbyang ba bya ba la yongasu 
10b6(khri pa): gcod pa'i shes pa gang yin pa ste/de ni chos shes pa zhes bya'o//de la rjesu rtogs pa'i shes pa gang zhe na/mig ma rtog go zhes bya bar shes pa gang yin pa dang /de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce 
10b7(khri pa): dang /lus dang /yid dang /gzugs dang /sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos rnams mi rtag go/zhes bya bar shes pa gang yi pa ste/de ni rjesu rtogs pa shes pa 
10b8(khri pa): zhes bya'o//de la pha rol gyi sems shes pa zhes bya'o//sems nyid kyis sems can gzhan rnams dang /gang zag gzhan rnams kyi sems dang /sems las byung ba'i chos rnams la the tsom med 
11a1(khri pa): par shes pa ste/de ni pha rol gyi sems shes pa zhes bya'o//de la kun rdzob shes pa gang zhe na/lam shes pa gang yin pa ste/de ni kun rdzob shes pa zhes bya'o//de las sdug 
11a2(khri pa): bsngal shes pa gang zhe na/sdug bsngal skye ba dang /gnas pa shes pa ste/de ni sdug bsngal shes pa zhes bya'o//de ka kun 'byung shes pa gang zhe na/kun 'byung ba spong ba shes pa gang yin pa ste/
11a3(khri pa): de ni kun 'byung ba shes pa zhes bya'o//de 'gog pa shes pa gang zhe na/sdug sngal 'gog pa shes pa gang yin pa ste/de ni 'gog pa shes pa zhes bya ba'o//de la lam shes pa gang zhe na/
11a4(khri pa): 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa shes pa gang yin pa de/de ni lam shes pa zhes bya'o//de ni lam shes pa gang zhe na 'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug 
11a5(khri pa): rnams zad par shes pa gang yin pa ste de ni zad pa shes pa zhes bya'o//de dang mi skye ba shes pa gang zhe na/srid pa'i 'gro ba rnams su mi skye bar shes pa ste/de ni mi skye ba 
11a6(khri pa): shes pa zhes bya'o//de la ji lta ba bzhin shes pa gang zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi ye shes gang yin pa ste/de ni ji lta ba bzhin shes pa zhes bya'o//de las mi 
11a7(khri pa): shes pa kun shes par byed pa'i dbang po gang zhe na/gang zag slob pa mngon par rtogs par ma gyur pa rnams kyi dad pa'i dbang po dang /brtson 'grus kyi dbang po dang /dran pa'i dbang po dang /ting nge 
11a8(khri pa): 'dzin gyi dbang po dang /shes rab kyi dbang po gang yin pa ste/de ni mi shes pa kun shes par byed dbang po zhes bya'o//de la kun shes pa'i dbang po gang zhe na gang zag slob pa mngon pa rtogs 
11b1(khri pa): par gyur pa rnams kyi dad pa'i dbang po dang /brtson 'grus kyi dbang po dang /dran pa'i dbang po dang /tinge 'dzin gyi dbang po dang /shes rab kyang /
11b2(khri pa): de la kun shes dang ldan pa'i dbang po gang zhe na/gang zag mi slob pa 'di lta ste/dgra bcom pa rnams dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' sa bcu pa la gnas pa rnams dang /
11b3(khri pa): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi 'dod pa'i dbang po dang //brtson 'grus kyi dbang po dang /dran pa'i dbang po dang /tinge 'dzin gyi dbang po dang /shes 
11b4(khri pa): rab kyi dbang po ste/de ni kun shes pa dang ldan pa'i dbang bo zhes bya'o//de la rtog pa dang bcas dpyod pa dang bcas pa'i tinge 'dzin gang zhe na/'dod pa dag las dben pa/sdig pa 
11b5(khri pa): mi dge ba'i chos dag las dben pa/dga' ba dang bde ba can bsam gtan dang po bsgrubs te/gnas pa ste/de ni rtog pa dang bcas pa dpyod pa dang bcas ba'i tinge 'dzin ces 
11b6(khri pa): bya'o//de la rtog pa med la dpyod pa tsam gyi tinge 'dzin gang zhe na/bsam gtan dang po dang /bsam gtan gnyis pa'i bar gang yin pa ste/de ni rtog pa med la dpyod pa tsam gyi tinge 'dzin ces bya'o//
11b7(khri pa): de la mi rtog mi dpyod pa'i tinge 'dzin gang zhe na/bsam gtan dang po nas bzung ste/'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched kyi bar du snyoms par 'jug pa ste/de ni mi rtog mi dpyod pa'i tinge 'dzin 
11b8(khri pa): ces bya'o//de la zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mched brgyad gang zhe na/'di lta ste/nang gzugs su 'du shes pas/phyi rol gyi gzugs chung ngu kha dog bzang po dang /kha dog ngan pa rnams la lta zhing /
12a1(khri pa): gzugs de dag zil gyis mnan nas shes/zil gyi mnan nas mthong ste/de lta bur 'du shes par gyur pa 'di ni zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mched dang po'o//nang gzugs med 
12a2(khri pa): par 'du shes pas phyi rol gyi gzugs chen por gyur pa/kha dog bzang po dang /kha dog ngan pa rnams lta zhing /gzugs de dag zil gyis gnon nas shes/zil gyis mnan nas mthong ste/
12a3(khri pa): de lta bur shes par gyur pa 'di ni/zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mche gnyis pa'o//nang gzugs med par 'du shes pas/phyi rol gyi gzugs sngon po kha dog sngon po sngon po lta bur ston pa 'od sngon po 
12a4(khri pa): 'byung ba rnams la blta ste/dper na zar ma'i me tog gam/yul wA ra Na si'i ras phun sum tshogs pa/kha dog sngon po/sngon po lta bur ston pa 'di sngon po 'byung ba de bzhin du/nang 
12a5(khri pa): gzugs med par 'du shes bas/phyi rol gyi gzugs/sngon po kha dog sngon po lnga bur ston pa 'od sngon po'abyung ba/rnams la lta ba 'di ni/zil gyis gnonapa'i skye mched 
12a6(khri pa): gsum pa'o//nang gzugs medapar 'du shes pas/phyi rolagyi gzugs ser po kha dog ser po lta bur ston pa 'od ser por 'byung ba rnams la blta ste dper na dong kha'i me tog gam/yul 
12a7(khri pa): wA ra na si'i ras phun sum tshog sa pa/ser po kha dog ser po/ser po lta bur ston pa 'ad ser po 'byung ba de bzhin du nang gzugs med par 'du shesapas phyi rol gyi gzugs ser po kha dog gser po 
12a8(khri pa): lta bur ston po 'od ser po 'byung ba rnams la blta ba 'di ni zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mched bzhi pa'o//nang gzugs med par 'dushes pas phyi rol gyi 
12b1(khri pa): bur ston pa 'od dmar po 'byung ba rnams la blta ste/dper na ban du dzi ba'i me tog gam/yul wa ra na si'i ras phun sum tshogs pa dmar po//
12b2(khri pa): nang gzugs med par 'du shes pas/phyi rol gyi gzugs dmar po kha dog dmar po dmar po lta bur ston pa/'od dmar po 'byung ba rnam la lta ba 'di ni/zil gyis gnan pa'i skya mchad lnga pa'i//nang
12b3(khri pa): gzugs med par 'du shes bas/phyi rol gyi gzugs kar po kha dog dkar po lta bur ston pa 'od dkar po 'byung ba rnams la lta ste/dper na skaram pa sangs rgyas kyi mdog dkar po kha dog dkar po dkar po 
12b4(khri pa): lta bur ston pa/'od dkar po 'byung ba de bzhin du/nang gzugs med par 'dushes pas/phyi rol gyi gzus dkar po/kha dog dkar po dkar bo lta bur ston pa/'od dkar
12b5(khri pa): 'byung ba rnams la lta ste/de ni zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mched drug pa'o//de rnam pa thams cad du gzugs kyi 'du shes rnams las yang dag par 'das te/thogs pa'i 'du shes rnams nub par
12b6(khri pa): gyur cing /sna tshogs kyi 'du shes rnams yid la mi byed pas/nam mkha' mtha' yas so snyam nas/nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mched bsgrubs te gnas pa 'di ni/zil gyis gnon pa'i skye mchid bdun pa'o//
12b7(khri pa): de rnam pa thams cad du nam mkha' mtha' yas skye mchid las yang dag par 'das/rnam par shes pa mtha' yas so snyam nas rnam shes mtha' yas skye mched bsgrugs te/gnas pa 'di ni zil gyis gnon pa'i
12b8(khri pa): skye mched brgyad pa ste/de dag ni zil gyis gnon pa'i skya mched brgyad zhes bya'o//de la zad par gyi skye mched bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/zad par sa dang /zad par chu dang /zad par me dang /zad par rlung dang /
13a1(khri pa): @#//zad par nam mkha' dang /zad par sngon po dang /zad par ser po dang /zad par dmar po dang /zad par dkar po dang /zad par rnam par shes pa ste/de dag ni zad par
13a2(khri pa): gyi skye mched bcu zhes bya ste/khams thams cad sa'i khams su mos par byed de/khams thams cad kyang sa'i khams kho nar khams gcig tu 'gyur ro//khams thams cad chu'i khamasu mos par
13a3(khri pa): byed de/khams thams cad kying chu'i khams kho nar khams gcigi tu 'gyuro//khams thams cad me'i khams su mos par byed/khams thams cad kyang me'i khams kho nar khams gcagi tu 'gyur ro//
13a4(khri pa): khams thams cad rlung gi khamasu mos par byed de/kham thams cad kyang rlung gi khams kho nar khams gcing tu 'gyur ro//khams tham sa cad nam mkha'i khamasu mos par byed de
13a5(khri pa): de thams cad kyang nam mkha'i khams kho nar khams gcin tu 'gyur ro ro//khams thams cad sngon por mos pa byed de/de thams cad kyang sngon po nyid du 'gyuro//khams thams
13a6(khri pa): cad ser por mos par byed de/de thams cad kying ser por kho nar 'gyur ro//khams thams chad dmar por mos par byed de de thams cad dmar po kho nar 'gyuro//khams thams can dkar
13a7(khri pa): por mos par byed de/de na thams cad kyang dkar po kho nar 'gyur ro//kham thams cad rnam par shes par byed de/de thams chad kyang rnam par shes pa kho nar khams gcig tu 'gyuro//sa dang
13a8(khri pa): chu dang /rlung dang /nam mkha' dang /sngon po dang /ser po dang dmar po dang dkar po dang /rnam par shes pa thams cad kyang gcig kho nar 'gyur te/de dag ni bdag gi se mched dang /de dag ni
13b1(khri pa): zad par du skye ba rgyas par byed pas/de dag ni zad par gyi skye mched rnams shes bya'o//re re yang skye mched thams cad du 'gyur te/de'i phyir zad 
13b2(khri pa): bco brgyad gang zhe na/'di lta ste/nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /dan dam pa ste 
13b3(khri pa): dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang rang 
13b4(khri pa): bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong 
13b5(khri pa): pa nyid dang /dngos po me pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//de la nang stong pa nyid gang zhe na/nang gi chos rnams shes bya ba ni/'di lta ste/mig dang rna ba dang /sna dang lce dang /lus 
13b6(khri pa): dang yid de/de la ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir/mig ni mig gis stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin du ther zug tu gnas 
13b7(khri pa): pa med pa dang /'jig par med pa'i phyir/rna ba dang sna dang /lce dang lus dang /yid ni yid kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyi zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de'i phyir te/de ni nang stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la phyi 
13b8(khri pa): stong pa nyid gang zhe na/phyi'i chos rnams shes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/gzugs dang sgra dang /dri dang ro dang /reg pa dang chos rnams te de la ther zug tu gnas med pa dang /'jig pa ma med pa'i phyir/
14a1(khri pa): @#//gzugs rnams ni gzugs rnams kyis stong ngo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin du ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir 
14a2(khri pa): sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg pa dang /chos rnams ni chos rnams kyis stong ngo/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rag bzhin de yin pa'i phyir/de ni phyi stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la phyi nang stong pa nyid gang zhe na/phyi nang stong ba nyid 
14a3(khri pa): gang zhe na/phyi nang gi chos rnams shes bya ba ni nang gi skye mched drug dang /phyi'i skye mched drug dang /de la ther zug tu gnas pa medapa dang /'jig pa med ba'i phyir nang gi chos rnams ni nang gi chos rnams kyis stongo//
14a4(khri pa): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rab bzhin de yin pa'i phyir ro //ther zug tu gnas pa medab dang /'jig pa med pa yin pa'i phyir/phyi'i chos rnams kyang phyi'i chos rnams kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na 
14a5(khri pa): de'i rang bzhin de yin ba'i phyir te/de ni phyi nang stong pa nyid ces bya'o/de la stong pa nyid stong pa nyid gang zhe na /ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang 'jig pa med pa'i phyir/chos thams cad kyi stong 
14a6(khri pa): pa nyid gang yin pa'i stong pa nyid des kyang chos thams cad kyi stong ba nyid stong ngo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te/de ni stong ba nyid stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la chen po stong pa nyid gang zhe na/
14a7(khri pa): ther zug tu gnas pa med ba dang /'jig pa med da'i phyir/shar phyogs ni shar phyogs kyis stong ngo//de ci'i phyir zhen/de'i rang bzhin de yi phyir ro//de bzhin du ther zug tu gnas pa medapa dang /
14a8(khri pa): 'jig pa med pa'i phyir /lho dang nub dang byang dang /phyogs mtshams bzhi te/phyogs de brgyad kyang de bzhin no//steng gi phyogs ni steng gi phyogs kyis stong ngo //
14b1(khri pa): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te/de ni chen po stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la don dam pa stong pa nyid gang zhe na/don dam pa stong pa nyid ces bya
14b2(khri pa): de la ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir mya ngan las 'das pa ni mya ngan las 'das pas stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i yang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir/de ni don dam pa stong pa nyid//de 
14b3(khri pa): de la 'dus byas stong pa nyid gang zhe na/'dus byas zhes bya ba ni/'dod pa'i khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /gzugs med pa'i khams te/de la ther gzugs tu gnas pa med pa dang/'jig
14b4(khri pa): pa med pa'i phyir/'dod pa'i khams de 'dod pa khams kyis stong ngo //de bzhin du gzugs kyi khams ni gzugs kyi khams kyis stong ngo //gzugs med pa'i khams ni gzugs med pa'i khams
14b5(khri pa): kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyi zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te/de ni 'dus byas stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid gang zhes na/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid/zhes bya ba 
14b6(khri pa): ni/chos gang la skye ba med pa dang /gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa dang /gzhan du 'gyur ba med pa ste/de la ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jag pa med pa'i phyir/'dus ma byas ni 'dus
14b7(khri pa): ma byas kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyer zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de'i yin pa'i phyir te/de ni lus ma byas stong pa nyid ces bya ba'o//de la mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid gang zhe na/ther zug tu gnas pa med dang /
14b8(khri pa): 'jig pa med pa'i phyir/chos gang la skye ba shin tu mi dmigs pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin du de yin pa'i phyir te/de'i mtha' las 'das pa stong nyid ces bya'o//de la thog ma dang tha ma med pa
15a1(khri pa): stong pa nyid gang zhe na/ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir/chos gang la 'ong ba mi dmigs pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te de ni thog ma dang 
15a2(khri pa): tha ma med pa stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la dor ba med pa stong pa nyid gang zhe na/thar zug tu gnas pa med dang /'jig pa med pa yin pa'i phyir/chos gang la chos gang yang dor du med pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na de'i rang 
15a3(khri pa): bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te/de ni dor ba med pa stong pa nid ces bya'o//de la rang bzhin stong pa nyid gang zhe na ther zug tu gnas pa med de/'jig pa med pa'i phyir 'dus byas sam/'dus ma 
15a4(khri pa): byas kyi chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin gang yin pa de ni/nyan thos rnams kyis ma byas/rang sangs rgyas kyis ma byas/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis ma byas/sangs rgyas 
15a5(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis ma mdzad pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhes na/de'i rang bzhin de ni yin pa'i phyir te/de ni/rang bzhin stong pa nyid ces bya'o/de la rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid gang zhe na/
15a6(khri pa): rang gi mtshan nyid ces bya ba la/gzugs kyi mtshan nyid ni gzugs su rung ba/tshor ba'i mtshan nyid ni myong ba/'du shes kyi mtshan nyid ni/kun tu shes par byed pa/'du byed rnams kyi mtshan 
15a7(khri pa): nyid ni/mngon par 'du byed pa/rnam par shes pa'i mtshan nyid ni/rnam par shes par byed pa ste/'dus byas kyi chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid gang lags pa dang /'dus ma byas kyi chos rnams 
15a8(khri pa): kyi mtshan nyid gang yin pa'i bar du de bzhin du sbyar te/ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir mtshan nyid de dag thams cad rang gi mtshan nyid kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang 
15b1(khri pa): bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te/de ni rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la chos thams cad stong pa nyid gang zhe na/chos thams cad ces bya ba ni phung po lnga dang /skye mched bcu gnyis dang /khams bco 
15b2(khri pa): brgyad dang /chos gzugs can rnams dang /gzugs med pa'i chos rnams dang /'dus byas kyi chos rnams dang /'dus ma byas kyi chos rnams te/de la ther zug tu gnas pa med pa dang /
15b3(khri pa): 'jig pa med pa'i phyir/chos thams cad chos thams cad kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i rang bzhin de yin pa'i phyir te de ni chos thams cad stong pa yid ces bya'o//de la mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid 
15b4(khri pa): gang zhe na/de yang ther zug tu gnas pa medapa dang /'jig pa med pa'i phyir/chosathams cad mi dmigs pa gang yin pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i bzhin de yin pa'i phyir ste/
15b5(khri pa): de ni mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid/ces bya'o//de la dngos po med pa stong pa nyid gang zhe na gang la dngos po 'ga' yang mi dmigs pa ste/de ni dngos po med pa stong pa ni nyid ces bya'o//
15b6(khri pa): de la ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid gang zhe na/'dus pa las byung ba'i ngo bo nyid gang yang med pa ste/de ni ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de la dngos po mad pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /gang zhe na/'dus pa las
15b7(khri pa): byung ba'i ngo bo nyid 'ga' yang med pa ste/de ni dngos po medapa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid/ces bya'o/shA radwa ti'i bu gzhan yang dngos po ni dngos pos stong ngo //dngos po med pa ni dngos po med pas 
15b8(khri pa): stong ngo //ngo bo nyid ni ngo bo nyid kyis stong ngo //gzhan gyi dngos po ni gzhan gyi dngos pos stong ngo //de la dngos po gang zhe na/dngos po zhes bya ba ni phung po lnga rnams te/'di lta ste/gzugs 
16a1(khri pa): dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa ste/de la dngos po ni dngos pos stong ngo //ji ltar na dngos po med pa ni dngos po med pas stong zhe na/dngos 
16a2(khri pa): po med pa zhes bya ba ni/'dus ma byas pa ste/de la 'dus ma byas ni/'dus ma byas kyis stong ngo //de bzhin du dngos po med pa ni/dngos po med pas stong ngo //ji ltar na/ngo bo nyid ngo bo nyid kyis 
16a3(khri pa): stong zhe na/chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni/shes pas kyang ma byas/mthong bas kyang ma byas/gang gis ma byas pa ste/de ni ngo bo nyid kyis stong pa zhes bya'o//ji ltar 
16a4(khri pa): na gzhan gyi dngos po gzhan gyi dngos pos stong zhe na/gang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams byung yang rung /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ma byung yang rung /chos rnams kyi chos gnas pa nyid dang /
16a5(khri pa): chos kyi dbyings dang /chos skyon med pa nyid dang /de bzhin nyid dang /phyin ci log pa de bzhin nyid dang /gzhan ma yin pa de bzhin nyid dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' ni gnas pa nyid 
16a6(khri pa): de/chos 'di dag gi gzhan gyis stong pa gang yin pa de ni/gzhan gyi dngos po gzhan gi dngos pos stong pa zhes bya ste/shA ra dwa ti'i bu gang la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos 
16a7(khri pa): mngon par zhen par mi bya ba'i chos thams cad ni de dag yin no//mngon par zhen pa med pa ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i pha rol tu son pa yin zhing /myur du bla na med pa yang dag par 
16a8(khri pa): rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'o////shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa khri pa las/gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po rdzogs s+ho////
16b1(khri pa): bam po gnyis pa//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bus la bka' stsal pa/shA radwa ti'i bu de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin 
16b2(khri pa): gshegs pas gnas la yang gnasu yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so//gnas ma yin pa la yang gnas ma yin par yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhIn du rab tu shes pa dang /'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa 
16b3(khri pa): dang /da ltar byung ba'i las rnams dang /las yang dag par len pa rnams kyi rnam par smin pa gnas kyi sgo dang /rgyu'i sgo nas yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhi du rab tu shes pa dang 'jig rten du mkhas sna tshogs 
16b4(khri pa): yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhinadu rab tu shes pa dang des sems can gzhan dag dang /gang zag gzhan rnams kyi mos pa sna tshogs nyid dang /mos pa du ma nyid yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhid du rab 
16b5(khri pa): tu shes pa dang /des sems can gzhan dag gang zag gzhan rnams kyi dbang po mchog dang /mchog ma yin pa nyid yang dag pa ji lta bu bzhin du rab tu shes pa dang/thams cad 
16b6(khri pa): du 'gro ba'i lam yang dag pa ji lta ba ba zhin du rab tu shes pa dag dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag gi yan lag dang /rnam par thar pa dang /bsam gtan dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms 
16b7(khri pa): par 'jug pa'i kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba dang ldan pa shes pa/yang dag pi ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes pa ba dang /sngon gyi gnas rnam pa dum rjes su dran pa dang /sems can rnams 
16b8(khri pa): kyi 'chi 'pho dang /skye ba yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhIn du rab tu shes pa dang /zag pa rnams zad pas zag pa med par sems rnam par grol ba dang /shes rab rnam par grol ba che/'di nyid la rang gi mngon par 
17a1(khri pa): @#//
17b1(khri pa): bram ze 'am/lha 'am/bdud dam/tshangs pa 'am/sus kyang 'jig rten na/chos dang mthun par ma bskor ba bskor zhes bya ba dang /ngas bar du gcod pa'i chos gang dag bshad pa de dag bstan 
17b2(khri pa): kyang bar du gcod par mi 'gyur te/de ni gnas med do zhes dam bcas na 'dir dge sbyong ngam/bram ze 'am/lha 'am/bdu dam/tshangs pa 'am/su yang rung ba 'ga' zhig 'jig rten na/chos 
17b3(khri pa): dang 'thun par bdag la rgol ba'i rgyu de yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ste/ngas rgyu de yang dag par rjesasu ma mthong bas bde ba thob ste/gnas shing bag tsha ba med pa thob la mi 'jigs pa thob ste/gnas 
17b4(khri pa): kyi khyu mchog rlabs po che'i gnas su dam bca'o//'khor gyi nang du seng ge'i sgra yang dag par bsgrag go/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo sngon dge sbyong ngam/bram ze'am/lha 'am/
17b5(khri pa): bdud dam tshangs pa 'am/sus kyang 'jog rten du chos dang mthun par ma bskol ba bskor ro zhes bya ba dang /ngas nyan thos rnams las 'phags pa nges par 'byin pa/nges par 'byed 
17b6(khri pa): pa de byed pa/sdug bsngal yang dag par zad par 'gyur ba'i lam gang bshad pa bsgrubs na/nges par 'byin pa ma yin zhing /sdug bsngal yang dag pa zad par mi 'gyur te/de ni gnas 
17b7(khri pa): med do zhes dam bcas na 'dir dge sbyong ngam/bram ze 'am/lha 'am/bdud dam/tshangs pa 'am/su yang rung ba 'ga' zhig 'jog rten na chos dang 'thun par rgol ba'i rgyu de yang dag par rjes su 
17b8(khri pa): mi mthong ste/ngas rgyu de yang dag par rjesasu ma mthong bas/bde ba thob ste/gnas shing bag tsha ba med pa thob la/mi 'jigs pa thob ste/gnas kyi khyu mchog rlabs po che'i gnas su dam bca'o//
18a1(khri pa): 'khor gyi nang du seng ge'i sgra yang dag par bsgrag go/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo sngon dge sbyong ngam/bram ze'am/lha'am/bdud dam/tshangs pa'am/sus kyang 
18a2(khri pa): 'jig rten du chos dang mthun par ma bskor ba'i bar du bskor ro zhes bya ste/de dag ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi zhes bya'o//de la so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/don so so yang dag par 
18a3(khri pa): rig pa dang /chos so so yang dag par rig dang /nges pa'i tshig so so yang dag par rigs pa dang /spobs pa so so yang dag par rig ste/de dag ni so so yang dag par rig bzhi zhes bya'o//
18a4(khri pa): de la byams pa chen po gang zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa sems can thams cad la dgra bo dang mdza' bshes la 'dra bar mdzad pa gang yin pa ste/de ni byams pa chen po zhes bya'o//de 
18a5(khri pa): la snying rje chen po gang zhe na/sems can yod pa ma yin pa'i sems can rnams la byams pa mi gtong ba gang yin pa ste/de ni snying rje chen po zhes bya'o//de la sangs rgyas kyi 
18a6(khri pa): chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa la 'khrul pa med pa dang /bco med pa dang /bsnyel ba med pa dang /tha dad pa'i 'du shes med pa dang /
18a7(khri pa): sems mnyam par ma bzhag pa med pa dang /so sor ma brtags pa'i btang snyoms med pa dang /'dun pa nyams pa med pa dang /brtson 'grus nyams pa med pa dang /dran pa nyams pa med 
18a8(khri pa): pa dang /ting nge 'dzin nyams pa med pa dang /shes rab nyams pa med pa dang /rnam par grol ba nyams pa med pa dang rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba nyams pa med pa dang lus kyi 
18b1(khri pa): las thams cad ye shes sngon du 'gro zhing /ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang ba dang /ngag gis las thams cad ye shes kyi sngon du 'gro zhing /ye shais kyi rjes su 'brang ba dag/yid kyi las thams cad ye shes
18b2(khri pa): sngon du 'gro zhing ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang pa dang /'das pa'i dus la ma chags ma thogs pa'i ye shes mthong ba 'jug pa dang /ma 'ongs pa'i dus la ma chags pa'i ye shes mthong pa 'jug pa dang /
18b3(khri pa): da ltar byung ba'i dus la ma chags ma thos pa'i ye shes mthong ba 'jag pa ste/de dag ni sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad ces bya'o//de la thams cad shes pa nyid gang zhe na/nyan thos dang rang sangs 
18b4(khri pa): rgyas rnams skye mched bcu gnyis po dag la gang zag la bdag med par phyogs gcig shes pa gang yin pa ste/de ni thams cad shes pa nyid ces bya'o//de la lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid gang 
18b5(khri pa): zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' theg pa gsum gyi lam shes pa dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pas dag par mi sems kyi/byang chub sems dpa'i lam gyi nges par 'byung 
18b6(khri pa): ba gang yin pa ste/de ni lam gyi rnam pa shes pa yid ces bya'o//de la rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gang zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pas dus gsum po dag tu rnam pa thams cad du chos thams cad 
18b7(khri pa): la the tshom med par mkhyen pa gang yin pa ste/de na rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid ces bya'o//de la pha rol tu phyin pa drug gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /tshul khrims kyi 
18b8(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dag brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ste/de dag ni pha rol tu phyin pa 
19a1(khri pa): phyin pa drug ces bya'o//de la mngon par shes pa drug gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bya ba shes pa mngon sum du bya ba mngon par shes pa dang /lha'i mig shes pa mngon sum du
19a2(khri pa): bya ba shes pa'i mngon par shes pa dang/lha'i rna ba'i shes pa mngon sum du byab shes pa'i mngon par shesapa dang /pha rol gyi sems shes pa mngon sum du byang ba shes pa'i mngon par zhes pa dang /
19a3(khri pa): sngon gyi gnas rjesu dran pa shes pa mngon sum du byad ba shes pa'i mngon par shis pa dang /zag pa zad pa shes pa mngon sum dubya ba shes pa'i mngon parshes pa ste/de dag ni mngon par shes pa drug
19a4(khri pa): ces bya'o//de la mig lnga gang zhe na/'di lta ste sha'i mig dang /lha'i mig dang /shes rab kyi mig dang /chos kyi mig dang /sangs rgyas kyi mig ste/de dag ni mig
19a5(khri pa): lnga zhes bya'o//de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyes bu chen po 'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bco ma ldan 'das ni zhabs shin tu gnas pa dang ldan pa yin no//
19a6(khri pa): de la zhabs shin tu gnas ba ni 'di yin te/zhabs kyi 'og gi mthil gnyis thams cad kyis sa la kun tu reg pa ste/dper na/za ma tog gi zhabs mnyam zhing zhabs phya nge ba sa gzhi shin tu mnyam pa
19a7(khri pa): la gzhag na/'og gi zhabs thams cad kyis sa la gun tu reg par 'gyur pa de bzhin du/bcom ldan 'das ni zhabs shig tu gnas pa dang ldan pa yan no//bcom ldan 'das ni mu khyud khor yug
19a8(khri pa): dang ldan pa yin no//de la mu khyud khor yug dang ldan pa ni 'di yin te/zhabs kyo 'og gi mthil gnyis la 'khor lo rtsibs stong dang ldan pa/lte ba dang bcas pa/mu khyud dang bcas pa/rnam pa
19b1(khri pa): thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa/mdzes par skyes pa gang dag yin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das ni phyag dang zhabs 'jam zhing gzhon sha chags pa yin no//de la phyag dang zhabs 'jam zhin gzhon sha chags pa 
19b2(khri pa): nyid ni 'di yin te/dper na shing pa la gyi 'da' ba 'am/ras bal gyi 'da' ba lta bu ste/ji ltar mi gzhan dag la med pa lta bu yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni sor mo ring ba yin no//de la sor mi 
19b3(khri pa): ring ba nyid ni yin te/de'i phyag dang zhabs kyi sor mo dag lhag par ring ba yin te/ji ltar mi gzhan dag pa med pa lta bu yin no/bcom ldan 'das ni phyag dag zhabs dra ba dang ldan pa yin te/de'i 
19b4(khri pa): phyag dang zhabs dra ba lhag pa dag skyes te/ji ltar mi gzhan dug la med pa lta bu yin do//bcom ldan 'das ni rting pa yangs pa dang ldan pa yin no//de la rting pa yangs pa ni 'di yin te/
19b5(khri pa): de'i zhabs kyi rting pa gnyis kyi mthil ni yangs pa ste/ji ltar mi gzhan dagltar mi gzhan dag la medapa lta bu yin no//bcom dan 'das ni dangs pa dang ldan pa yin//de la rting pa yang dag pa ni 'di yin te/
19b6(khri pa): zhabs long mo'i tshigs mi mngon pa nyid ni 'di yin te/rting pa yangs pa la yang dag par sbyor/rting pa yangs pa yang zhabs long mo'i tshigs mi 'don pa la sbyor ba ste/ji ltar mi gzhan dang la 
19b7(khri pa): med pa de lta bu yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni/byin pa ri dwags e na yI lta bu dang ldan pa yin no/de la byin pa ri dwags e na'i lta bu ni 'di yin te/de byin pa byin gyis phra bar skyes shing /byin gyis 
19b8(khri pa): phra bar yang dag par byung ba ste/dper na/ri dwags kyi rgyal po e na yI lta bu yin no//bcom lan 'das ni bzhengs bzhin du ma btud par phyag dbus mor sleb pa yin no//de la bzhengs bzhin 
20a1(khri pa): du ma btud par phyag dbus mo la sleb pa ni 'di yin te/bcom ldan 'das ni bzhengs na sku ma btud par phyag gi mthil gnyis kyis rang gi dbus mo'i lha nga yang dag par nges par 
20a2(khri pa): mdzad cing kun tu nyug par mdzad pa yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni 'doms kyi sba ba sbubs su nub pa yin no//de la 'doms kyi sba ba sbubs ni nub pa nyid ni 'di yin te/dper na rigs btsun pa'i glang po che cang shes 
20a3(khri pa): sam/rta cang shes kyi lta bu yin no//bcom ldan 'da'as ni spu re re nas mdzes par skyes shing /g.yas su 'khyil pa yin no//de la spu re re nas mdzes pa skyes shing /g.yas su 'a khyil pa nyid 
20a4(khri pa): ni 'di yin te/de'i spu'i khung bu re re nas/spu re re mthon mthing lcang lor 'khyil ba 'jam zhing /snum la g.yas su 'khyil cing /mdzes par skyes pa yin no//bcom ldana'adas 
20a5(khri pa): ni sku'i sbu gyen du phyogs pa dang ldan pa yin no//de la sku'i spu gyen du phyogs pa nyid ni 'di yin te/de'i sku dang dbu las skyes pa'i dbu skra dang spu rnams ni kha gyen du bltas 
20a6(khri pa): shing /mthon mthing la kun nas lcang lor 'khyil pa 'jam zhing /snum la g.yos su 'khyil pa mdzes par skyes pa yin na/bcom ldan 'das ni lpags pa srab cing 'jam la snum pa yin no//de la 
20a7(khri pa): lpags pa srab cing 'jamal snum pa ni 'di yin no//de'i sku la chu 'am/rdul kun tu mi chags shing mi gnas pa yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni mdog gser lta bu dang ldan te/dper na 'dzam bu'i chu 
20a8(khri pa): bo'i gser gyi mchod sdong la rin po che'i sna tshogs kyis spras pa lta bur gzugs bzang zhing mdzes la blta na sdug pa yin te/des ni gser gyi kha dog lta bu zhes bya'o//bcom ldan 'das ni bdun mtho 
20b1(khri pa): ba yin no//de la bdun mtho ba nyid ni 'di yin te/de'i zhabs gnyis kyi gong mtho ba gnyis gzugs bzang zhing mdzes la blta na/sdug pa sha dang khrag gis yongasu rgyas par gyur pa dang /de bzhin du phyag
20b2(khri pa): gnyis kyi mtho ba gnyis gzugs bzan zhing mdzes la blta na sdug pa sha dang khrag gis yod su rgyas par gyur pa dang/thal gong gnyisu gnyis mtho bar gyur pa dang /gnya' gong du mtho ba gcig 
20b3(khri pa): gzugs bzang zhing mdzesal blta na sdug pa sha dang/khrag gis yongasu rgyas par gsur pa yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni dpung mgo shin tu zlum pa dang ldan pa yin no//bcom ldana'adas ni thal gong
20b4(khri pa): rgyas pa dang ldan pa yin no//bcom ldan 'das ni sku shin tu drang por 'khrungs pa yin no//bcom ldanas 'das ni sku na ya gro da ltar chu zheng gab pa yin no//de la n+ya da ltar sku chu zheng gab pa

32a8(khri pa): chos su btags pa tsam du zad/chos su btags pa de la ni skye ba med 'gag pa med de/ming gis brdas tha snyad du 'dogs nas kho nar zad la/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan gsum cu rtsa gnyis kyi ming de dag kyang nang na dang

32b2(khri pa): chos su btags pa de la ni skye ba med/'gag pa med de/ming gi brdas tha snyad du 'dogs pa kho nar zad la/dpe byad bzang po'i ming de dag kyang nang na yang med/phyi rol 
32b3(khri pa): na yang med/gnyi ka'i ba ra na yang mi gnas so/shA ra dwa ti'i bu/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pas/ming gi brdas btags pa dang /chos su btags 
32b4(khri pa): pa la de ltar bslab par bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems/'chen pos ming gi brdar btags pa'i chos khong du chud par bya'o//des ming gi brdar brtags pa'i chos khong du chud bas/gzugs 
32b5(khri pa): la mngon par zhen par mi byed/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'a du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa la mngon par mi byed do//de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar la mngon par zhen par

57b1(khri pa): dwags sam/rnam par byang ba'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg pa dang /
57b2(khri pa): chos rnams kun nas nyon mongs pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/rnam par bya ba'i tshig bla dwags byang  chub sems dpa' yin snya ma mam/gsol pa/btsu pa bcom ldan 'das ma  lagaso//shA ra dwi ti'i bu 'di 
57b3(khri pa): ji snyam du sems/gzugs rnams skye ba'i tshig bla dwags sam/'gag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chu sems dpa' yin snyam ma ma/gsol pa/btsun ba bcom ldan 'das ma lags so//shA ra dwa ti'i bu'adi ji 
57b4(khri pa): snyam du sems/sgra dang/dri dang ro dang reg ba dang/chos rnams skye ba'i tshig bla dwags sam/'gag ba'i tshig bla dwags byang chub semas dpa' yin snyam mama/gsol pa/btsun  pa bcom 
57b5(khri pa): ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di  ji snyam du sems/gazugs rnams dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/dangos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang ch+rub sems dpa' yin snyam
57b6(khri pa): mam;gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg pa dang /chos rnams dngos po yod pa' tshig bla dwags sam/dngos po med pa'i tshig bla
57b7(khri pa): dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa btsom ldan 'das ma lagaso/shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'id snyam du sems/mig gi khams kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dba' yin
57b8(khri pa): snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'dadas ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam su sems/gzugs kyi khams dang/mig gi rnam par shes ba'i khams kyi tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sem+sa
58a1(khri pa): @#//dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar gyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa'
58a2(khri pa): yin snyam mam/gsol ba/btsun pa bcom ldan 'a das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams
58a3(khri pa): tshig bla dwags sam/mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chubasems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsal pa/btsun pa bcom lan 'das ma lagaso//de bzhin du sbyar te/yid gyi rnam par shes pa'i khamskyi bar gyi tshig bla dwagsbyang chub sems dpa'
58a4(khri pa): rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mig rtag pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lags so//de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par she sa pa'i khams kyi bar
58a5(khri pa): mig gi khams dang /gzungs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams nas/de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar gyi bde ba'i tshig 
58a6(khri pa): bla dwags sam/sdug bsngal ba'i tshig bla dwags byang chu sems dba' yin snyam mam/gsol pa btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/mig gi khams
58a7(khri pa): dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gyi rnam par shes pa'i kham sa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/yud kyi rnam par shes pa'i zhams kyi bar gyi bdag yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/bdag med
58a8(khri pa): pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'dasam lagaso//shA radwa ti 'i bu 'di ji snyam du sem/mig gi khams dang /gzags kyi khams 
58b1(khri pa): dang|mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khamasakyi bar du stong pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/bcug
58b2(khri pa): pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes ba'i khams dang /de bzhinadu sbyar te/yid kyi rnam
58b3(khri pa): par shes pa'i khams kyi bar gyi mtshan ma yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mtshan ma med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyamamam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagos//
58b4(khri pa): shA radwa ti'i bu 'di jisnyam du sems/mig gi khabs dang /gazugs kyi khams dang /rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /de bzhin du sbyar /yid kyi rnam par shesapa'i khams kyi bar
58b5(khri pa): du smon pa yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/smon pa med pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dpa' yinasnyam mam/gasol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa
58b6(khri pa): ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du semams/mig gi khams dang/gzugs kyi khams dang/mig gi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khamsdang /de bzhin du sbyarate/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams
58b7(khri pa): kyi bar gyi zhi ba'i tshig bla dwags sam/ma zhi ba'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dba' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsunpa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ja snyam du sems/
58b8(khri pa): mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar gyi dben pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mi dben
59a1(khri pa): @#//pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol ba/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/mig gi khams dang /gzugs
59a2(khri pa): kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khamasakyi bar du kun nas nyon mongs pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/rnam par byang ba'i tshig bla dwags byang
59a3(khri pa): chub sems dpa' yin snyamamam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'da'as ma lagaso//shA radwati'i bu/'di ji snyam du sems/migagi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rna par shes
59a4(khri pa): pa'i khams dang /de bzhinadu sbyar te/yid kyi rnam pa shes pa'i khams kyi bar gyi skye ba'i tshig bla dwags sam mi skye ba'i tshig bla dwags byang chung sems dpa' yin snyam ma gsol pa/
59a5(khri pa): btsun pa bcom ldan 'dasam lagaso//shA radwati'ibu/'di ji snyam du sems mig gi khams dang /gzu gas kyi khams dang /migagi rnam par shes pa'i kham sa dang /de
59a6(khri pa): bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyis rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar gya dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun
59a7(khri pa): pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA ra dwati'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/ma rig pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar gyi tshig bla dwags byang chubasems dpa' yin snyam mam gsol pa/btsun
59a8(khri pa): pa bcomaldan 'das ma lagaso//shwa radwati'i bu 'di ji snyam dusems/ma rig pa dang de bzhin du sbyar te/rga sha'i bar gyi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems

59b3(khri pa): pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/bdag med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sesams dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan das ma lagaso//shA ra dwa ti'i bu/'di ji snyam du sesam/ma rig pa dang /
59b4(khri pa): de bzhin du sbyar te/rga sha'i bar gyi stong pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mi stong pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dpa' yin snyom mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'dis ma lagaso//shA radwa
59b5(khri pa): ti'i bu/'di ji snyam du sems/mi rig pa dang /de bzhin tu sbyar te/rga shi'i bar gyi mtshan ma yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam mtshan ma med pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chu ba sems dpa' yin snyam
59b6(khri pa): mam/gsol ba/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//shA radwa ti'i bu/'di ji snyam dusems/ma rig pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar kyi smon pa yod pa'i tshig bla dwags
59b7(khri pa): smon pa med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bom ldan 'das ma lagaso/shA radwa ti'i bu/'di ji snyam du sems/mi rag pa dang /de bzhin dusbyar te/
59b8(khri pa): rga shi'i bar gyi zhi ba'i tshig bla dwags sam/ma zhi ba'i tshig bla dwags byang chu ba sems dpa' yid snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan las ma lagaso//shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'di ja snyam
60a1(khri pa): du sems/ma rig pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar gyi dben pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mi dben pa'i tshig bla dwag byang chub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/gsol ba/
60a2(khri pa): btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lags so//shA rwa ti'i bu/'di ji snyam du sems/ma rig pa dang de bzhin du sbyar te/rga sha'i bar gyi kun nas nyon mongs pa'i tshig bla dwags sam rnam par byang ba'i tshig 
60a3(khri pa): bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' yi snyam mam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das pa lags so//shA rwa tri'i bu/'di ji snyam du sem/ma rig pa dang /de bzhIn du sbyar te/rga shi'i ber 
60a4(khri pa): gyi skye ba'i tshig bla dwags sam/'gag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems/chub sems dpa' yin snyam/gsol pa/btsun pa/bcom ldan 'das ma lags/shA rwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du 
60a5(khri pa): sems/ma rig pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/rga she'i bar gyi dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang cub sems dpa' yin snyam mam/
60a6(khri pa): gsol pa/btsun pa bcom dpa' 'das ma lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA rwa tri ti'i bu la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//shA rwa ti'i bu khyod kyis don 
60a7(khri pa): gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na/'di skad du gzugs kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub kyi sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa'i tshig bla dwags 
60a8(khri pa): byang chub sems dpa' ma yin/gzugs rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags sam mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags/byang chub sems dpa' ma ma yin/de bzhin du tshorab dang /'du shes dang 'du shes dang /'du shes dang /
60b1(khri pa): rnam par shes pa'i tshig bla dwags sam/mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du gzugs bde ba'i tshig bla dwags ma yin/sdug bsngal ba'i tshig bla dwags ma yi na/
60b2(khri pa): bdag yod pa'i tshig bla dwags ma yin na/bdag med pa'i tshig bla dwags mayin/stong pa'i tshig bla dwags ma yi/mi stong pa'i tshig bla dwags yin/mtshan ma yod pa'i tshig bla dwags
60b3(khri pa): yin/mtshan ma med pa'i tshig bla dwags mi yin/smon pa yod pa'i tshig bla dwags pa yin/smon pa med pa'i tshig bla dwags/zhi ba'i tshig bla dwags ma yis/ma zhi ba'i tshig bla dwags
60b4(khri pa): ma yin/dben pa'i tshig bla dwags ma ma yin/mi dben pa'i tshig bla dwagasam yin/kun nas nyon mongs pa'i tshig bla dwags ma ba yin/rnam par byang ba'i tshig bla dwags ma min/skye 
60b5(khri pa): ba'i tshig bla dwags ma yin/'gag pa'i tshig bla dwags ma pa yin/dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags dang /bo med pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dpa' ma yin/
60b6(khri pa): de bzhin du sbyar te/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla drags dang /dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwagsbyang chub sems dpa' ma ma yin/de bzhin
60b7(khri pa): du mig gi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' ma min/de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid kyi tshig bla dwags byang chubsems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du mig rtag pa 'am/mi 
60b8(khri pa): rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chubasems dpa' ma yin/de bzhIn du rna ba dang /sna dang/lce dang /lus dang yid rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags dang/mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwagsbyangchub sems dpa' ma yin
61a1(khri pa): de bzhin du sbyar te/mig dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags dang /dngos po med pa'i bar gyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang lce dang /
61a2(khri pa): lus dang /yid dngos po yod pa'i tshig blo dwags dang /dngos po'i med pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub kyi sems dpa' ma yin/gzugs rnams kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub/de bzhin du sgra dang dri dang /
61a3(khri pa): ro dang reg pa dang /chos rnams kyi tshig bla dwags/byang chub sems dpa' ma yi ni/gzugs rnams rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags dang mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags/byang chub/sems dpa' ma yin/de lta bu ni sgra dang dri dang /
61a4(khri pa): ro dag reg pa dang /chos rnam rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags ba'i tshig bla dwags dang ma rtag pa'i tshig bla dwag byang chub sems dpa' ma ma yin/de bzhin du sbyar te/gzugs rnams dngos po yod pa'i tshig ba dwags dang /
61a5(khri pa): dngos po med pa'i bar gyis tshig bla dwags byang chub ma yin/de bzhin du sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg pa dang /chos rnams/dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags 
61a6(khri pa): dang /dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags/ba dwags byang chub sems dpa' ma yan/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub/
61a7(khri pa): sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams/khams kyi bar gyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems kyi khams dpa' ma yin/mig gi khams dang gzugs kyi khams dang mig gi rnam 
61a8(khri pa): par shes pa'i khams rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags/mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub kyi sems dpa' ma yin/de lta bu ni bar gyi tshig bla dwags/de bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i/khams kyi bar gyi tshig ba dwags 
61b1(khri pa): byang chub sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin du sbyar te/mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub 
61b2(khri pa): sems dpa' ma yin/debazhin du/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams ngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dang /dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags byag chub sems dpa' ma yin/ma rig pa'i tshig 
61b3(khri pa): bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' ma yi na/de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar gyi tshig bla dwags byang chub semasadapa' ma yin/de bzhin tu ma rig pa rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags byang cub sems dpa' 
61b4(khri pa): ma yin/ma rig pa mi rtag pa'i tshig bla dwagasabyang chub sems dpa' ma min/de bzhin du sbyar te /rga shi'i bar gyi tshig blang dwags byang chub sems pa dang /mi 
61b5(khri pa): ma rig pa rtag pa'i tshig ba dwags sam/mi rtag pa'i tshig/bla dwags byang chub sems ma yin/de bzhin du/sbyar te/rga shi'i bar du rtag pa'i tshig bla dwags/de bzhin du 
61b6(khri pa): brtag pa'i tshig bla dwags kyang chub sems dpa' ma yin/de bzhin/de bzhin du sbyar ste/ma rig pa dngos po yod pa'i tshig bla dwags dang /dngos po med pa'i tshig bla dwags kyi bar du byang chub sems dpa'
61b7(khri pa): ma yin/de bzhin du sbyar te/rga shi'i bar dudangos po yod pa'i tshig ba dwags dang /dngos po mad pa'i tshig bla dwags byang chub semasadapa' ma yin/zhes zer de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /
61b8(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus/'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs gtan mi dmigs lags na/gzugs kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub/sems
62a1(khri pa): @#//dpar 'gyur ba lta ga la mchis/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa yang de lta lagaso//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/
62a2(khri pa): gzugs rtag pa 'am/mi rtag pa gtan mi dmigs lags na/gzugs kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dpa' 'gyur ba lta ga la mchis/tshor ba dang /'du she dang /'du byed
62a3(khri pa): rnams dang /rnam par shesab yang de lta lagaso//btsug pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs bde zhes bgyi ba 'am/sdug bsngal zhes bgyi ba gtan mi dmigs lags /gzugs 
62a4(khri pa): kyi tshig bla dwags byang chung sems dpa'ar 'gyur ba lta ga la mi cha/tshor ba dang /'du shis dang /'du byed rnamsa dang /rnam par shes ba yang de lta lags so//btsun pa 
62a5(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das/gzug stong zhes bgyi ba 'am/mi stong zhes bgyi ba gtan mi dmi gsalags na/gzugs kyi tshig bla drags byang chub sems dpar 'gyur ba lta ga la 
62a6(khri pa): mchis/tshor ba dang /'angu shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par she ba yang de lta lagaso//btsan pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs mtshan ma mchis zhes bgyi ba 'am/mtshan 
62a7(khri pa): ma ma mchis zhes bgyi ba gtan mi da migs lags na/gzugs kyi tshigs bla dwags byang chub sems dpa'ar 'gyur blta ga la mchis/tshor ba dang /ngu shes dang /'angu byed rnams dang /
62a8(khri pa): rnam par shes pa yang de lta lagaso//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs smon pa mchis zhes bgyi ba 'am/smon pa ma mchis zhes bgyi ba ga tan mi dmigs lags na/gzugasakyi tshig bla
62b1(khri pa): dwags byang chub sems dpar 'gyur ba lta ga la mchis/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa yang de lta lagaso//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs zhi zhes 
62b2(khri pa): bgyi ba 'am/ma zhi zhes bgyi ba gtan mi dmigs lags na/gzugs kyi tshig bla dwags byang chub sems dbangar 'gyur ba lta ga la mchi sa/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /
62b3(khri pa): rnam par shes pa yang de blta lagaso//btsun pa bco ldan 'das/gzugs dben zhes bgyi ba 'am/mi dben zhes bgyi ba gtan mi dmigs lags na/gzugs kyi tshig bla dwagasabyang chub 
62b4(khri pa): sems dpar 'gyur ba lta dag la mchis/tshor ba dag/'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa yang de lta lagaso//btsun pa bcam ldan 'das/gzugs 
62b5(khri pa): kun nas nyon mongs zhes bgyi ba 'am/rnam par byang ba zhes bgyi ba gtan mi dmigs pa lags na/gzugs kyi tshi bla dwags byang chub semasadapar 'gyur ba lta ga la mchis/
62b6(khri pa): tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnam dang /rnam par shes payang de lta lagaso//btsun pa pa bcom ldan 'das/gzugs

69a5(khri pa): pa la gzhi'i don med do/shA radwa ti'i bu de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'achen po shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la gnas pa'i bya chub sems dpa'i gzhi'i dod med do//shA radwa ti'i bu de bzhin
69a6(khri pa): ti'i bu dper na/de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rjogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi mig la gzhi'i don med do/de bzhin du yid kyi bar la gzhi'i don med do/she radwa ti'i bu de bzhin byang 
69a7(khri pa): chub sems dpa' sems dang 'chen po shes rab kyi pha ralatu phyin pa la gnas pa'ibyang chub sems dpa'i gzhi'i don med do//she radwa ti'i bu dper na/de bzhIn gshegs pa dag bcom yang dag par 
69a8(khri pa): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nang stong pa nyid la spyod pa la gzhi'i don med do//de bzhin du sbyar te/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar la spyod pa la gzhi'i don de med do//shA radwi ti'i bu de bzhin du byang chag
69b1(khri pa): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i gzhi'i don med do//shA ra dwa ti'i bu dper na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgya
69b2(khri pa): kyi dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la gzhi med do//de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar la gzhi'i don med do//shA ra dwa ti'i bu de bzhin du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che po

69b4(khri pa): byas kyi khams kyi gzhi'i don med do//bA ra dwi ti'i bu/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnad pa'i byang chub sems dba'i gzhi'i don med do//
69b5(khri pa): shA ra dwi ti'i bu dper na/skye ba med pa la gzhi med do//de bzhin du 'gag pa med pa dang /mngon par 'du bya ba med pa dang /'byung ba med pa dang /dmigs su med pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs
69b6(khri pa): pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa la gzhi med do //shA ra dI ti'i bu/de bzhin du byung chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gzhan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i gzhi'i
69b7(khri pa): don med do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus/'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das gang gi skye ba ma mchis pa la gzhi ma mchis pa lags/gang gis
69b8(khri pa): kun nas nyon mongs pa ma mchis pa'i bar la gzhi ma mchis pa lag/gang gi rnam par byang ba ma mchis pa'i bar la gzhim mchis pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu
70a1(khri pa): @#//gzugs kyi skye ba med pa la gzhi med do//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa'i skye ba med pa la gzhi med//de bzhin du sbyar te/

70b8(khri pa): 'jug pa po dang /byed pa po dang /slong ba po dang /kun nas slong ba po dang /tshor ba po dang /tshor bar byed pa po dang /shes pa po dang /mthong pa po rnams shin tu rnam par dag par la gzhi med do//

71a2(khri pa): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor shar ba la mun pa'i gzhi med do//shA radwi ti'i bu/de bzhin du/byang chub sem dpa' sems dpa' chen po/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la gnas pa'i byang chu sems dpa'i gzhi'i

71b3(khri pa): med pa/bstan tu med pa/thogs pa med pa ste/'di lta ste/mtshan nyid med par mtshan nyid gcig pa yin pas/shA radwa ti'i bu byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen pos/chos thams cad la ma

74a2(khri pa): zhe na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/'di ltar 'di lta ste/nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga po dag/sgyu ma'i skyes bu lta bur rig par bgyi ba'i slad du'o//shA radwa ti'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/'o na nye bar len pa'i phung

74a7(khri pa): pa'i ngo bo nyid de yang dmigasu ma mchis pa'i sladadu'o//bka' stsal pa/shwa radwa ti'i bu/'di ji snyam du sems/phung po lnga po dag brag cha lta bu yin snyam mam/de bzhin du mig yor lta bu dang /

154a3(khri pa): nyid kyi bar dang /dngos po med ba'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid gyi bar gyi phyir/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du med na/de la byis pa so so'i skye bo ma rig pa dang /sred pa'i dbang gis mngon par 
154a4(khri pa): zhen pa de dag gis mngon par zhen nas/yod pa ma yin pa la yod par brtags so//de dag ni 'di lta ste/rtag pa dang chad pa'i mtha' gnyis la chags pas de dag ni mi shes mi mthong ba ste/chos

154a6(khri pa): la mngon par zhen to//de dag mngon par zhen pa nyid gang yin pas na/yod pa ma yin pa'i chos rnams la yongs su rtog par byed pa'i phyir/de dag ni mi shes mi mthongs ba dag yin no//de dag ci zhig mi 
154a7(khri pa): shes mi mthong zhe na/gzugs mi shes mi mthong ngo //de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa mi shes mi mthong ngo /de bzhin du sbyar te//sangs rgyas kyi 
154a8(khri pa): chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du mi shes mi mthong ngo /gang gi phyir mi shes mi mthong ba de'i phyir/byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams shes bya ba'i grangs su 'gro'o//de dag ni nges par 'byung ba ma yin te/

154b4(khri pa): pa stong pa nyid du yid mi skyes pa nas/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyi bar la yid mi ches so//yang de dag ni mi gnas pa dag yin te/gang la mi gnas zhe na/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la mi gnas
