210b2(khri pa): brgyad pa'i bar du chen por mi bgyis chung ngur mi bgyid/de zhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu chen por mi bgyid chung dang dur mi bgyid pa nas de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar dag chen 
210b3(khri pa): por mi bgyid chung dur mibagyid/byang chub chen por mi bgyid chung bur mi bgyid/sangs rgyas chen por mi bgyid chung bar mi bgyid pa lagaso/gzhan yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni gzugs 
210b4(khri pa): sdud par mi bgyid 'phro bar mi bgyid/de bzhin du tshor ba dang 'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa sdud par mi bgyid/'phro bar mi bgyid pa nas/de bzhIn du sbyar te/
210b5(khri pa): sangs rgyas kyi bar du sdud par mi bgyid 'phro 'tshar bar mi bgyid la/gso/gzhan yang shes rab kyang pha rol tu phyin pa ni zug sa tshad mchis par mi bgyid tshad ma mchis par mi bgyid pa nas/
210b6(khri pa): de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du tshad mchis par mi bgyid/tshad ma mchis par mi bgyi lagaso/gzhan yang shes bar kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni/gzugs yangs par mi bgyid dog par mi bgyid/
210b7(khri pa): de bzhin du tshor ba dang/'du shes dang/'du byed rnams dang/rnam par shes pa yangs par mi bgyid dogs par mi bgyid/de bzhin du/sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi ba ra du yangs par mi bgyid dog ar mi bgyid/
210b8(khri pa): lagaso/gzhan yang shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni gzugs stobs dang ldan par mi bgyid/stobs chung bar mi bgyid/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang/'du bye rnams dang/rnam par
211a1(khri pa): shes pa stobs dang ldan par mi bgyid stobs chung bar mi bgyid pa nas/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du stobs dang ldan par mi bgyed stobs chung bar mi bgyid
211a2(khri pa): lags so//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das rnam grangs des na 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi pha rol tu phyin pa chen po lags so//btsun pa bcom
211a3(khri pa): ldan 'das gal te theg pa la gsar du zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la brten/de bzhin du bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha 
211a4(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la brten nas/'di snyam du shes te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu yin pa ni gzugs 
211a5(khri pa): chen por bgyid pa'am chu dur bgyid pa'o//de bzhin du tshe rab dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa chen por bgyid pa'am chu ngur bgyid pa'o//de bzhin
211a6(khri pa): du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du chen por bgyid pa'am chungur bgyid pa'o//de bzhIn du sbyar te/gzugs stobs dang ldan par bgyid pa 'am stobs chung ba'i bar du bgyid pa'o//de bzhin tu tshor ba dang /
211a7(khri pa): 'du shes dang /'du byed dang rnams dang /rnam par she ba stobs dang ldan par bgyi ba 'am/stobs chung bar bgyid pa'o snyam du de lta bu'i 'du shes su gyur na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa'
211a8(khri pa): sems dpa' chen po de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i rgyu 'thun pa ma lags so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/gang gzugs che bar bgyid pa 'am chung bar bgyid pa 'am/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyis bar du
211b1(khri pa): che bar bgyid pa'am chung bar bgyid pa de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i rgyu 'thun pa ma lags//gang gzugs stobs dang ldan par bgyid pa'am stobs chung bar bgyid pa'am/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /

211b3(khri pa): stobs dang ldan par bgyid ba 'am stobs chung bar bgyidapa 'am stobs chung bar bgyi ba 'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa rgyu mthun pa ma lags/gang gzugs chen por bgyid pa 'am chu ngur bgyid pa 'am/de bzhin du gang tshor ba dang /
211b4(khri pa): 'du shes bar bgyid pa 'am chung bar bgyid pa 'am stobs chung bar bgyed pa 'am stobs chung ba 'am/de bzhin du gang 'gyur ba 'am stobs chung bar
211b5(khri pa): bgyid pa 'di ni de'i dmigs pa chen po lags pa'i slad du'o//de ci'i slad du zhe na/dmigs pa'i 'du shes can rnams la ni/byang chub gang yang ma mchis pa'i slad du'o//de ci phyir zhe na/'di 
211b6(khri pa): ltar sems can ma skyes pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ma skyes par blta bar bgyi'o//de bzhin du gzugs ma skyes pa'i slad du shes par bgyi pha rol tu phyin pa ma skye par blta bar bgyid/de bzhin 
211b7(khri pa): du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa ma skyes pa'i slad du shesarab kyi pha rol tu phyin par ma skyes par blta bar bgyed/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du ma skyes pa'i 
211b8(khri pa): slad du shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa ma skyes par lta bar bgyi/sems can ngo bo nyid ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kya pha rol tu phyin pa ngo bo nyid ma mchis par blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/
212a1(khri pa): sangs rgyas kyi bar du ngo bo nyid ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ngo bo nyid ma mchis par blta bar bgyi/sems can stong pa nyid kyi slad du shes rab kyi pha ro la tu 
212a2(khri pa): phyin pa stong pa nyid du blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du gzugs stong pa nyid kyi slad du she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin par stong pa nyid du blta bar bya/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par 
212a3(khri pa): shes pa stong pa nyid kyi slad du shis rab kyi pha rol tu phyin na stong pa nyid du blta bar gyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du stong pa nyid kyi slad du/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na stong pa nyid du blta 
212a4(khri pa): bar bgyi/sems can dben pa nyid kyi slad du shes rim gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dper na pa nyid du blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du gzugs dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes pa dang /'du byed rnams la dang /rnam par shes pa dben pa'i slad du 
212a5(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin dben pa nyid du blta bar babagyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/sang rgyas kyi bar du dben pa nyid kyi sla/da du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa nyid du blta 
212a6(khri pa): bar bgye/sems can ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa ma mchis pa nyid du blta bar bgye/de bzhin du gzugs dang tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes 
212a7(khri pa): pa ma mchis pa'i slad du she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ma mchis pa ni de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du ma mchis pa'i slad du shes/rab kyi pha rol tu phyin na ma mchis pa nyid du bltar bar bgyi/sems 
212a8(khri pa): can bsam gyis mi khyab pa nyid kyi slad du shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa nyi shu blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du gzugs dag tshor ba dang /'du shes pa dang /'du byed rnams la dang /rnam par shes pa dang /
212b1(khri pa): pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa nyid kyi slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa nyi du blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du bsam gyis mi khyab pa nyidakyi slad du shes rab kyi pha
212b2(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa bsam gyis ni khyab pa nyid du blta bar bgyi/sems can 'jig pa ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa 'jig pa ma mchis par blta bar bgyi/de bzhinadu gzugs dang /
212b3(khri pa): tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa 'jig pa mchis pa'i slad du shes par blta bar bgyi sems can mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba ma mchis pa'i slad du shas rab kyi pha rol
212b4(khri pa): rgyas kyi bar du 'jig pa ma mchis pa'i slar du shes rab kyi pha rol/tu phyin pa 'jig pa ma mtshis par blta bar bgyi semasacan mngon par du shes dang /'du byed dang /'du byed rnams dag rnam par shes pa mngon
212b5(khri pa): tu phyin pa mngon par rdzogs par 'tshad rgya ba ma mchis par lta bar bgyi/de bzhin du gzugs dang /tshor ba dang /'adu shes dang /'du byed pa rnams dang /rnam par zhes pa mngon
212b6(khri pa): par rdzogs par 'tshang rgyab ma mchis pa'i slad du she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par rdzog par 'tshang rgya ba ma mtshis par blta bar gyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du mngon
212b7(khri pa): par rdzogs par 'atsang rgya ba ma mchis pa'i sla du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin mngon par rdzogs par tshang rgya ba ma mchis par blta bar bgyi'o//sems can stobs dang mi ldan pa'i slad du shes rab kyi
212b8(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa stobs dang mi ldan par blta bar bgyi/de bzhin gzugs dang /tshor ba la/'du shes dang /'du byed rnams nga /rnam par shes pa stobs dang mi ldan pa'islad du shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa
213a1(khri pa): stobs dang mi ldan par blta bar bgyi/de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi bar du stobs dang mi ldan pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stobs dang mi ldan par ba lta
213a2(khri pa): bar bgyi ba'i slad du ste/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das rnam grangs des na 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi pha rol tu phyin pa chen po lagaso//de nas 
213a3(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bos 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'dasaji ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phin pa'i ming yongasu bsgrags pa de ltar sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i ming yongasu 
213a4(khri pa): bsgrags pa ma mdzad la/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pas dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mi yongasu bsgrags 
213a5(khri pa): par ma mdzad lagaso//btsun pa bcom ldan das de bzhin du sbyar te/ji ltar shes rab kyi pha rolag tu phyin pa'i ming yang yo ngas su bsgrags pa ma mdzad pa de ltar sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres
213a6(khri pa): pa bco brgyad kyi bar gyi ming yongasu bsgrags pa ma mdzad lagaso//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun dga'i bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kun dga' bo shes rab kyi pha 
213a7(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa ni pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po dag gi sngon du 'gro ba yin zhing /de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dris pa bco brgyad kyi bar gyi sngon du 'gro ba yin pa'i phyir ro//kun dga' bo 'di ji snyam du sems 
213a8(khri pa): rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongasu ma bsngos pa'i sbyin pa sbyin pa'i bar du phyin pa'i grangasu 'gro snyam 'am/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lagaso//kun dga' bo 'di dza snyam du sems 
213b1(khri pa): tshul khrims dang /bzod pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bsam gtan dang /shes rab rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su ma bsngos pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i grangs su 'gro snyam 'am/gsol pa/

213b3(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid du yongs subasngos na sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa zhes bgyi ba'i grangs su mchi ba nas shes rab kyi bar du rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid du yongs su bsngos na shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa zhes bgyi ba'i grangs
213b4(khri pa): su mchi lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo sbyin pa gnyis su med pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyen/pa nyid du yongs su bsngos pa ni/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin zhes bya ba'i 
213b5(khri pa): grangs su 'gro'o//de bzhin du sbyar te/shes rab kyi bar du gnyis su med pa'i tshul gyi rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su/bsngos na shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa zhes bya ba'i grangs su 'gro'o//
213b6(khri pa): de bzhin du skyes pa med pa'i tshul dang /dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa tham cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongasu bsngos na shes rab kyi phyin pa zhes bya ba'i grangasu 'gro'o////
213b7(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa khri pa//bam po bcu bdun pa//gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar sbyin pa gnyis su ma mchis pa'i tshul dang /de bzhin du skye ba ma mchis 
213b8(khri pa): pa'i tshul dang /dmigs su ma mchis pa'i tshul gyis thams cad mkhyen pa nyid tu yongs su bsngos na sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa zhes bya ba'i gangs su mchis lags/de bzhin du ji ltar shes rab kyi bar du gnyis 
214a1(khri pa): mchis pa'i tshul dang /de bzhin du skye ba ma mchis pa'i tshul dang /dmigs su ma mchis pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngos na shes rab kyi pha rol

214a4(khri pa): 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/ji ltar gzugs ni gzugs kyis stong /de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shesadang /'du byed rnams dag rnam par shes pa ni rnam par shes pas stong pa nas de bzhin du 
214a5(khri pa): sbyar te/byang chub kyi bar ni byang chub kyis stong ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar pha rol tu phyin pa dang gzugs de ni gnyis su med cing gnyis su dbyer med la/de bzhin du shes rab kyi pha 
214a6(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa dang /byang chub kyi ba de ni gnyis su med cing /gnyis su dbyer med pa'i phyir ro//kun dga' bo de lta bas na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po 'di dag gi sngon du 'dren pa yin pa 
214a7(khri pa): nas de bzhin du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar gyi sngon du 'dren pa yin no//dper nas chen po las sa bon gang dag btab pa dag ni tshogs pa rnyed nas skye ste sa chen po ni de dag gi 
214a8(khri pa): rten yin no//kun dga' bo de bzhin du pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po'i 'di dag ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la brten nas byung ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu 'dzin pa ni de dag gi rten yin no//de bzhin du shes 
214b1(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brten nas/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi 'byung ba nas/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du 'byung zhing /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du 'byung ngo //kun dga' 
214b2(khri pa): bo rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid la brten pa ni pha rol tu phyin pa lnga dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dris pa bco brgyad kyi bar dag yin te/kun dga' bo de lta bas na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po de dag gi 
214b3(khri pa): yongs su 'dren pa yin zhing /de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dresapa bco brgyad kyi bar dag gi yongs su 'dren pa yin no//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' 
214b4(khri pa): bos 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo de shes 
214b5(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par bgyid pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa na yon tan gang dag yongs su 'dzin pa'i yon 
214b6(khri pa): tan de dag thams cad ni bka' ma stsal lags ste/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa bzung ba dang /tshul bzhin du yidal bgyid pa'i bar du bgyis na/dge ba bcu'i las 
214b7(khri pa): kyi lam rnams 'jig rten du 'byung lags so//de bzhin du bsam gtan bzhi dang /tshad med pa bzhi dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco 
214b8(khri pa): brgyad kyi bar dag 'jig rten du 'byung lagaso//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das zhes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bzung ba dang /tshul gzhin du yid la bgyid pa'i bar du bgyi na rgyun tu zhugs pa 'jig rten 
215a1(khri pa): na mchis lags so//de bzhin du lan cig phyir 'ong ba dang /phyir mi 'ong ba dang /dgra bcom pa dang /rang sangs rgyas 'jig rten na mchis lags so//bcom ldan 'das shes 
215a2(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bzung ba dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa'i bar du bgyid na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams 'jig rten du 'byung ba lags so//de skad 
215a3(khri pa): ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kun dga' bo rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo gang gis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i 'di thos pa 
215a4(khri pa): nas tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa'i bar du byas pa de la ni yon tan de dag 'ba' shig tu zad par nga mi smra'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/kun dga' bo rigs kyi bu'am/rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab 
215a5(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par byed pa dang /tshul bzhin du yi+ed la byed pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems dang yang 
215a6(khri pa): bral bar mi 'gyur ba de ni tshul khrims kyi phung po tshad med pa dang ldan par 'gyur/de bzhin du gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'tshang ba dang /klog pa dang/kun chub par byed pa dag 
215a7(khri pa): tshul bzhin du yid la byed ni/ting nge 'dzin gyi phung po dang /shes rab kyi phung po dang /rnam par grol ba'i phung po dang /rnam par 'grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phung po tshad med pa dang /ldan par 'gyur ro//
215a8(khri pa): kun dga' bo rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa ba dang /kun chub par bye pa dang /tshul bzhin du yang la byed pa dang /rnam pa 
215b1(khri pa): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems dang yang mi 'bral bar 'gyur ba de ni de bzhin gshegs par rig par bya'o//kun dga' bo nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi tshul khrims kyi phung po dang /
215b2(khri pa): tinge 'dzin gyi phung po dang /shes rab kyi phung po dang /rnam par grol ba'i phung po dang /rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phung po gang yin pa nyan thos dang /rang sang rgyas thams cad kyi 
215b3(khri pa): tshul khrims kyi phung po nas rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong pa'i bar de ni/kun dga' bo rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo de'i tshul khrims kyi phung po dang /tinge 'dzin gyi phung po dang/shes rab kyi phung 
215b4(khri pa): po dang /rnam par grol pa'i phung po dang /rnam pa grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba yin pa'i tshul khrims kyi phung po nas/rnam par grol ba'i ye shes/mthong ba'i phung po'i brgya'i char yang nye bar mi 'gro

221a3(khri pa): chad sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod cing 'ongs pa yin/de bzhin du tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dag/bsam 
221a4(khri pa): gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyodacing 'ongs pa yin no//shA radwa ti bu de ni phyin chad de de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs 
221a5(khri pa): pa'i sangs rgyas grangs med tshad med pa dag la bsnyen bkur byed cing /'dir 'ongs pa yin no//shAradwa ti'i bu/byang chub kyi sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de ni shes rab kyi 
221a6(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa mthong ngam thos na/bdag gis ni ston pa mthong ngo zhes sems bskyed par 'gyur ro//shA radwa ti'i bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' sems dpa' 
221a7(khri pa): de la yang don dang /tshig las mtshan ma med pa dang /gnyis su med pa dang /dmigs su med pa'i tshul gyis rjes su rtogs par 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 
221a8(khri pa): 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di nyan pa po dang /lta ba po rnams kyi ston pa lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor 
221b1(khri pa): ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la ni nyan pa po dang /lta ba po med do//rab 'byor chos bems po nyid kyi phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 
221b2(khri pa): mi thos mi mthong ngo//de bzhin du chos bems po nyid kyi phyi ra/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi thos mi mthong ngo//de gzhin du chos bems po nyidakyi phyir/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu

221b4(khri pa): stong pa nyid mi thos mi mthong ngo/de bzhin du sbyar te/chos bems po nyid kyi phyiro dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar du mi thos mi mthong ngo//de bzhin du chos bems
221b5(khri pa): po nyid kyi phyir dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams mi thos mi mthong ngo/chos bems po nyid kyis phyir/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar du mi thos mi mthong nga ོ//
221b6(khri pa): de bzhin du chos bems pa nyid kyi phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu nas/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du mi thos mi 'thong ngo//rab 'byor chos bems
221b7(khri pa): po nyid kyis phyir byang chub dang /sangs rgyas kyang mi thos mi mthong ngo/denas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes 
221b8(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab pa mos par dka' ba lagaso//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
222a1(khri pa): phyin pa 'di ni zab bo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar gzugs ni ma bcings ma grol ba yin la/rab 'byor de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed 
222a2(khri pa): rnams dang /rnam par shes pa ni ma bcings ma grol ba'i phyir ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gzugs dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin ni gzugs yin pa'i phyir ro//rab 'byor de bzhin du tshol ba dang /

222a4(khri pa): de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyi na pa dngos po med pa'i rang bzhan ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin yin pa'i phyir ro//rab khyor de bzhin du tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin 
222a5(khri pa): pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni ma bcings ma 
222a6(khri pa): grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa'i bar du dngos po med pa'i rang ba bzhin ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa yin pa'i phyir ro/rab 'byor nang stong pa nyid ni ma bcings ma 
222a7(khri pa): grol pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na rab 'byor nang stong pa nyi dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin ni nang stong pa nyid yin pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin du sbyar te/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar du yang ma bcings ma 
222a8(khri pa): grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nid stong pa nyid dngos po med pa'i rang gzhin ni dngos po med pa'i ngo bo yid stong pa nyid yin pa'i phyi ro//rab 'byor dran pa nye bar gzhag pa 
222b1(khri pa): bzhi ni ma bcings ma grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin ni dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi yin pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin du sbyar te/rab 'byor 
222b2(khri pa): 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar dang /thams cad shes pa nyid dang /lam gyi rnam pa 
222b3(khri pa): shes pa nyid dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar ni ma bcings ma grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i dngos po med pa'i rang bzhan ni rnam pa thams cad

222b5(khri pa): rang bzhin ni gzugs yin pa'i phyir ro//rab 'obyor de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam pa ra shes pa'i sngon gyi mtha' ni ma bcing ma grol ba'o//
222b6(khri pa): de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'obyor rnam par shes pa'i sngon gyi mtha'adangos po medapa'i rang bzhin ni rnam par shes pa yin pa'i phyiro//de bzhin du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid kyi bar gyi sngon gyi mtha' ni ma 
222b7(khri pa): bcings ma grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/dbang 'byor sngon gyi mtha'adangos po medaba'i rang bzhin ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid yin pa'i phyir ro/rab 'byor gzhan yang gzungs ni sngon gyi mtha' las ma

223a1(khri pa): pa ni sngon gyi mtha' las ma bcings ma grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor sngon gyi mtha' dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin ni rnam par shes pa yin pa'i phyir ro//rab 'byor de bzhin 
223a2(khri pa): du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar ni sngon gyi mtha' las ma gcings ma grol ba'o//de ji'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor don gyi mtha' dngos po med dang /rang bzhin ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid

223b1(khri pa): par shes pa yin pa'i phyir ro//rab 'byor de bzhin du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du da ltar byung ba las ma bcings ma grol ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/da ltar byung ba'i dngos po med pa'i
